# FROM `state/chapter-summaries.md`, 3 SECTION(S), 32974 BYTES, MOVED VERBATIM
## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — THE FIVE CHAPTER SUMMARIES ABOVE CORRECTED, AND NOTHING BELOW THIS LINE IS A SUMMARY

**Appended downward and nothing above this line is edited, so the five summaries written by the writing run stand as it wrote them and this section is the correction to them. The word counts in the five headings below are the counts after the repair, measured per file with `sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//' file | wc -w`, and the counts in the headings above are the writing run's, and both are given because item 17 in `state/open-threads.md` is the finding that a figure a later writer inherits as a fact is a figure a later writer may have to re-measure.**

**Chapter 331 (1,828 before, 1,877 after) — the man of about thirty-four, a lane off the Slade, about a hundred yards of a bay, and the reason out loud in an empty lane.** The correction is in one paragraph and it is a change of person. **The summary above says he wanted the rest of a foreman's sentence. He did not. The half of a foreman's sentence that anybody holds is held by Rennick Adley, who keeps the books four hundred yards further up that same lane and has wanted the rest of it since the third month of the year after. The man of thirty-four has never had any of it, and the sentence that gave the distance — *the asking is not a thing that can be done from four hundred yards* — matched neither man's position, because he was standing about a hundred yards from the bay.** It now reads that the asking is not a thing that can be done from a hundred yards by a man with no office in a building that is not hers, and not from four hundred yards either, and that is the whole of the difference between the two men. **Everything else in the chapter stands: he stops at a hundred yards and does not go in, the door is open because of the weather, the fourth rule goes out loud in three turns to nobody, the two of seventeen are at the back of the bay and are not spoken to and are not going to start, the mark in chalk is a mark, the count of askings does not move, and the question mark is about a door and is in a mouth and is not put to a person about a person.**

**Chapter 332 (1,625 before, 1,630 after) — the bay, the foreman of fifty-one, and a day on which nobody asked anybody anything at all.** **The correction is in the closing paragraph and it is the same creditor: *a man with a fair hand four hundred yards off wanting half a sentence* is now *a man four hundred yards up the same lane who keeps the books wanting the rest of a sentence*, and the phrase is gone because both men in this matter have a fair hand and the phrase never disambiguated anything.** The three earlier paragraphs of the chapter named him correctly as the man who keeps the books and are untouched, and so is the whole shape of the chapter: nobody asks her about the box she has been in since the first week of the seventh month of the year after, the day he asks is the day she stops saying the half she has got, a thing that cannot be refused is not a question, about nine men know and not one has said a word, the man of about fifty-five with a chain is in the bay and is not one of the four, the two of seventeen are at the back, a man comes to have a length of wall measured and is gone inside a minute, and the question mark is about a mark on a wall.

**Chapter 334 (1,458 before, 1,466 after) — a house with a door on it four miles off, four steps, a fire that went out, and the reason he gave out loud about six weeks before he did the thing.** **The correction is the hinge and it is a repair of sense, not of plot. The chapter said *he is about to do a third thing* and then, in the next paragraph, *this is about six weeks before it, and the six weeks are made of three things* — and the three things listed are three places he has already been, and the *six weeks* belongs to the once he has already told her about, six weeks before the second thing, which is on the page at Chapter 0312 and Chapter 0321.** The chapter now says that what is between this and it is not made of anything she could ask about, and introduces the three as *the three places it goes through*: the room over the shop that sells tar, the bay about four hundred yards off it, and the two of seventeen at the back of that bay. **The not asking on her side is still the same not asking and not a different one; the speech on the step is unchanged word for word; the decision about whether she trusts his judgment is still unmade and still hers and is not made in this chapter; the fire, the lamp, the two plates upside down to drip and the question mark about the fire are all unchanged.**

**Chapter 336 (1,296 before, 1,295 after) — the ground-floor room off the old river road, eleven books under a window with the pane out of it, and the third of three absences said out loud to a page.** **The correction is one preposition. A sentence said *it is the last time in this volume that any of the three gets said out loud in a room*, and a narrator's reference to the volume is card language that the batch's own sweep, keyed on the string *the volume*, could not see, because *in this volume* does not contain it.** It now reads *it is the last time any of the three is going to be said out loud in a room*, and the meaning is the same and the phrase is not on the page. **Nothing else moved: she says the cost out loud and a man who has said a thing twice is findable by the saying of it, she wrote none of it down and said out loud that writing it down would make it an order, the third absence is that nobody can tell her a person is not coming, the finding on the shelf is still a question carrying no period with her own name at the foot, the carrier puts four on the shelf, the pane is still out, and the question mark is about a page.**

**Chapter 340 (1,500 before, 1,488 after) — the same room over the same shop, the lamp not lit, a nail in about four feet of wall, and what ten weeks of not asking have come to.** **Two corrections and one struck phrase, and the chapter's job does not change: it pays the rule and does not discharge it, it answers the volume's promise in the negative and in a room, and it hands on.** *One,* a sentence said *it is the last shape the finding takes **in this volume*** and is now *it is the last shape the finding takes*, for the reason given under Chapter 336. *Two,* the man of thirty-four was going to come nine miles **for the third time** and is going to come **for the second time**, because Chapter 0323 puts him in that room for the first time and Batch 0003's record says he was in it once, and the page governs over the outline; the same sentence's **about four minutes before it** became **about nine sentences**, which is the outline's own figure for the reason-speech, and Chapter 0323's five sentences were a different speech given afterwards and out of the room. *Three,* **the phrase *the two thirds are not one third* is struck**, because one of the two thirds was never a third, and in its place the chapter says that only one of the two men in that room has walked those nine miles this season, and the man of sixty-one walked them in an earlier month. **A fourth change was made in passing and was not in the review: a paragraph said the two men were not going to be in the room at the same time, four lines after a paragraph said one of them would be, and the outline requires both, so the false one is gone and what replaces it says they are in the room at the same time and nobody is in a position to arrange that either.** The rest of the chapter stands: the two people who said the rule out loud do not know each other exists and are not told; the answer is that a question cannot be asked in about nine hundred buildings at once by people who cannot be instructed to ask it; a class is a heading and a heading is a company; the count of askings is the same as it was ten weeks ago; the Lowcross bill, the clause, the figure at the end of the cold passage and the guarantee are all untouched and all unnamed in the right places; and the question mark is about a nail in a wall.

**The batch after the repair, and the gates.** **Ten chapters, 14,578 words, and the batch total moved from 14,529 because four sentences in four of them were corrected and one sentence was split in two.** The sentence bound holds, the sixty-word and forty-word windows are zero, the emphasis runs are eighty-eight and none of the ten is over a sixth on either joint, the question marks are one to a chapter, the `no form` frame is two to a chapter with zero collisions, the sixteen card-language forms are at zero and so are the two phrases the review added, and the count of askings is seven and did not move.

## VOLUME 07, BATCH 0005 — CHAPTERS 341 TO 350, THE FIFTH MOVEMENT, AND THE SMALLEST THING

**Chapter 341 (1,494) — the bay at the top of the lane off the Slade, a gate shut at about the fourth hour, and one man going up to a gate nobody asked him about.** Halla Wray, fifty-one, in a box since the first week of the seventh month of the year after, entered by a class, never asked. The man of about fifty-five with the chain, who is not one of the four who cannot make sense of a paragraph and says so, gets up at about the fifth hour and goes down the lane to a shut gate and comes back and says nothing about it. **She does not ask him, and nobody in that bay looks up.** The two of seventeen are at the back of the bay on their written engagement and are not spoken to, not thanked, not sent for, and not going to start. The second sentence she owes the man who keeps the books four hundred yards up the lane is not asked for. The mark in the chalk and the third link of the chain are about four feet apart and neither is going to become anything.

**Chapter 342 (1,589) — the counting room at the top of that lane, a shutter half up, a column with no heading over it, and a man of about thirty who says a true thing out loud and is not asked one word back.** Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, in the books since the second month of next year. The man of about thirty from the second gang stands in the doorway with a figure that is not the figure he was given and says so out loud, and Adley could have put one question there and does not, because a question would make him a party and a party is the only person a thing can be told what it is going to be used for. The piece of waste paper is still at the bottom of a box that is not the Company's. **The bill at Lowcross is named once in this batch and nowhere else in it: nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, on the third line of a district page that came up the lane with the gang's count and that nobody looked at.** The word standing against it is not the word that stands against nineteen days of a crew's money in a weigh-house ledger about half a mile off, and the two are not in one column.

**Chapter 343 (1,191) — the end of about four hundred yards of cold flags, a lock, a rack light by one, and a woman of about twenty-six who counts in her head and does not touch the rack.** She is not one of the three clerks and is not in this and has never been in it. **The chapter's finding is about the room and not about her: the rail room at the far end of that passage is not on the passage's work, nothing that comes down those flags goes into it, nothing that comes out of it comes up them, and that is the whole of why a thing said out loud in there stays in there.** Her figure has gone up and she is not saying by how much and is not going to. She has three questions for a man of sixty-one and has asked none of them, and the fact that he came at all gave her no fourth.

**Chapter 344 (1,205) — the counting room at the back of a dye end, a stove let go out, sixteen books, and a wage she is not going to ask the reason for.** Nell Kest, twenty, alone in the room all day. **The new beat in this chapter is that she is not going to ask the man who holds her wage what it is held against, and the reason is the volume's own: asking him would make the two of them parties, and a party is the only person a thing can be told what it is going to be used for, and the answer would be a record.** She is one of the people nobody has asked and there is nobody she can put one to. The two women she cannot reach are still two she cannot reach. The sixteenth book is not given to anybody and nobody has offered her a key. The notice in a coat is not sent. She is not going up this river this season and that is not the same as never, and she is the only person who knows it.

**Chapter 345 (1,117) — a house with a door on it four miles off, four steps, a woman at the bottom of them, and a man who is not going to tell her where he is going.** The man of about thirty-four is going to walk about nine miles on a day he is not paid for to a room at the end of a cold passage, and he has been in that room once, and he is not going to tell her. **The six weeks comes back in its own words and is not re-derived: the last time he did a thing of that kind he told her six weeks before he did it, out loud, on purpose, without asking her first, and the reason he gave her is still the truest thing he has ever said to her.** This time he is not telling her at all, and the not telling is the same absence as the not asking and is not a better way of doing anything. A woman of about thirty-five next door is unspoken to for about four lines and is a different person from every other woman of about thirty-five on the page. The bill for being findable is not his and stands on somebody else's account.

**Chapter 346 (1,425) — the room at the far end of the passage, a rail four feet off the floor, and nine minutes with a coat open and a hand that never went to the rail.** The second visit and not the third, on his own two feet, on a day he is not paid for. The man of sixty-one is on the wrong side of the rail and was standing there before the door gave, and **one of the two men walked the nine miles this morning and the other walked it in a month that is gone, and the one that did it this morning is not going to say which, and nobody in the room is going to tell him.** He has the asking ready and does not ask it. He says the reason out loud, standing, in about nine sentences, to the man of sixty-one and to a clerk of about fifty-five and to about four other people, and it is the finding: a question at that rail goes onto the shelf the sheets go on, a question anywhere else has to be carried to him, there are about nine of about nine hundred a year that are not as the person above wrote and he would tell the truth if anybody asked him, there is nowhere for a man to mark a disagreement, a question is a party, a party is a person, a person is one, about nine hundred of these go out of that room a year, what is available at that size is a class, a class is a heading, a heading is a company, and therefore a question cannot be asked in about nine hundred buildings at once by people who cannot be instructed to ask it. **The man of sixty-one does not answer and is not asked and is not thanked. The woman with the scuttle says nothing. The clerk writes none of it. The room does not have it afterwards and he does not tell anybody what was said in it.**

**Chapter 347 (1,089) — the ground-floor room at the top end of the same passage, a carrier up the flags, and four things entered correctly in a hand that does not vary.** Four days after the nine minutes. The clerk of about fifty-five enters four things correctly and writes nothing else, and nobody has asked her about the rest of it and nobody is going to. **The finding in this chapter is a thing she said out loud that she cannot check: in the ninth month she said at her own table that the man of thirty-four is not coming back, and nobody is going to tell her that he was in that room, and there is nothing in this empire that would let a clerk of an office be told that a person is coming or that a person is not.** She said in the ninth month that it was the last time any of the three would be said out loud in a room, and this afternoon she did not say it, and the not saying is what keeping it costs. The question on her shelf is still a question and carries no period.

**Chapter 348 (1,221) — the top of a stair in Auremar with no other way up, a fee board of four items, and a box of about four hundred bought and unfilled blanks under the counter.** Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, two years at the fourth counter. The undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended, unexercised for about two years, and a correct non-use is entered as one. **The inherited phrase is used once, in its own words and without a date computed from it: *a person who is not asked has not been given a duty*, and she has known that since about the seventh month of next year.** Nobody came up that stair wanting anything and she is not told which of the two that is going to be. The fourth thing on the fee board that three clerks cannot account for is hers and the two other clerks' and not the book-keeper's, and neither she nor the woman of thirty-four at the other end of the same counter said anything about it. The box is not a post and nobody asks for it. The line in her own book about the woman of thirty-four is a window and not a wall. The decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is unmade and is not made here.

**Chapter 349 (1,153) — the room over a chandler's shop, four steps, a table with nothing on it, and a lamp not lit at about the fifth hour.** Three days after the nine minutes. **He does not tell her what was said in that room and she is at the bottom of the four steps and does not ask him how it went, and there is no form for refusing to be told a thing and none for being entitled to insist on it.** He has a number in his head that is made up of people and not of sheets, and he cannot give it away because the giving of it would be a list and a list of persons is a company. He puts his hand flat on the table and the table has nothing on it and the lamp is not lit until about the seventh hour and is lit then by somebody else.

**Chapter 350 (1,214) — the same room a week later, the nail in about four feet of wall, and a man at a table with a number in his head that he cannot give away.** The last chapter of the volume. **The room is referred to and not described again, and the chapter is a list of what is still standing, and nothing in the list is settled by anything in it.** The who is not named. The second half of the question is not asked and the clause on a clerk's shelf is still a clause with no period. Both people stay in their boxes and the form that put them there is not asked to be void. The guarantee stands offered and unanswered with about sixty children inside it and is not printed in its own words. The bill at Lowcross stands unpaid at nineteen pounds three and fourpence with nobody liable. About nineteen people stay behind the bank. The four who cannot read a paragraph stay unasked, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain and the man of about sixty-one are neither of them one of the four. The two of seventeen stay unspoken to, unthanked and unsent for. The figure at the end of the cold passage is not handed on and does not get a heading. Nobody is thanked, nothing is resolved, the lamp is not lit, and the next asking is nobody's yet.

## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — THE SUMMARIES ABOVE CORRECTED AGAIN, AND NOTHING BELOW THIS LINE IS A SUMMARY

**Written downwards by the phase that reviewed Batch 0005 and repaired it. The ten summaries above are the writing run's record and are not rewritten; every correction is here, with the figure it replaces beside the figure that replaces it, and the eight chapters whose words moved are named one by one. This file takes summaries and nothing else.**

**Chapter 341 (1,494) — unchanged.** Nothing in the review touched it.

**Chapter 342 (1,589) — unchanged.** Nothing in the review touched it. **A forty-word window measured with punctuation kept returns zero in this file, and a second reading of the same test with punctuation normalised away returns one run of forty-nine words against closed Chapter 0333, and the run is Rennick Adley's own column in the second of the Company's books stated in the same words in three chapters of this volume, and it is named here and not hidden and it is not cut, because the same man saying the same thing about the same column in his own lane is the page and not a lift.**

**Chapter 343 (1,191 before, 1,195 after) — the end of the cold flags, a lock, and a woman who counts in her head.** The correction is the closing clause only. **It closed on a thirty-four-word string byte-identical to the closing clauses of Chapters 0344 and 0348, and the fact it carries is unchanged and the asking-count did not move: the count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is the same as it was when the first rack went down this morning, and it is going to be the same in ten weeks.** The rest of the chapter stands, including the finding that the rail room at the far end of the passage is not on the passage's work.

**Chapter 344 (1,205 before, 1,226 after) — the counting room at the back of a dye end, a stove let go out, sixteen books, and a wage she is not going to ask the reason for.** Two corrections. *One,* the closing clause, which was byte-identical to Chapter 0343's and Chapter 0348's, and now reads *the same as it was when the stove was let go out*. *Two,* and this is the one the review called the stacked locative: the closing movement of the chapter said her room is *about four hundred and thirty miles from a building off a road in another town where a woman nine miles off has told a different woman a different thing*, which piles three distances on one another and leaves the building unidentifiable in the same batch where Chapter 0350 put a different woman nine miles off that same building. **The paragraph now says the room is about four hundred and thirty miles from a building off the old river road in a town down this river, and that a woman nine miles off that lane stands at the end of a cold passage in that building and has told a different woman a different thing, and that neither woman has ever heard of the other one.** Nell Kest is still twenty, still in a furnace town, still with a wage held since the second week of the third month of the year after, still not going up this river this season, and still the only person who knows the difference between that and never.

**Chapter 345 (1,117 before, 1,121 after) — a house with a door on it four miles off, four steps, and a man who is not going to tell her where he is going.** Two corrections. *One,* the date line said the lamp *is not lit until about the seventh hour and was not lit then*, which asserts a past fact about an hour that has not arrived at the fifth hour; it now reads *is not lit and is not going to be lit until about the seventh hour*, which is what Chapters 0341, 0349 and 0350 say in the same room. *Two,* the closing clause, which was byte-identical to Chapter 0349's, and now reads *the same as it was when the lamp was not lit*. **The six weeks comes back in its own words in the body of the chapter and is not re-derived, and the bill for being findable is still not his, and nothing in the chapter moved except the two sentences above.**

**Chapter 346 (1,425 before and after) — the room at the far end of the passage, a rail four feet off the floor, and nine minutes with a coat open.** One correction and the word count does not move, because *nine* and *thirteen* are both one word. **The chapter said the reason-speech was *in about nine sentences* and the speech is nine quoted paragraphs carrying thirteen sentences, and this is the one chapter of the batch where the reader is invited to count, so the page now says *in about thirteen sentences*.** The nine came from `outline/volume-07.md` and out of Batch 0004's own repair, and **it is still printed in closed Chapter 0340, which says a man of thirty-four is going to say it *in about nine sentences*, and a completed batch's chapter is not reopened, so the two figures now stand apart on the page and the divergence is carried forward as an open item and is not reconciled by picking one.** Everything else in the chapter stands: the second visit and not the third, the declining to say which of the two men walked the nine miles that morning, the asking ready and not asked, the man of sixty-one not answering, the clerk writing none of it, and the room not having it afterwards.

**Chapter 347 (1,089 before, 1,104 after) — the ground-floor room at the top end of the same passage, a carrier up the flags, and four things entered correctly in a hand that does not vary.** Two corrections, and one of them was a chapter that contradicted itself two lines apart. *One,* the date line said the nine minutes were *four days* behind her; the batch's own card said *three days* and its own card title said *the same week*, and the calendar puts Chapter 0347 exactly one seven-day step after Chapter 0346, so the page now says *seven days*. **The 4/4 to 2/1 day-name jump at the month boundary is this volume's inherited four-week cycle and is not a defect and was not touched.** *Two,* the chapter said a man of about thirty-four was in that room *and she was in that room and she wrote none of it*, and then said two lines later *she is not told that he was there*, and she cannot be in a room and untold of it in the same page. **She is now in her own room at the top end of that passage at the time of it, and she wrote none of it, which is the volume's own phrase and is what the chapter means, and the beat that follows is untouched: a woman who said in the ninth month that he is not coming back and who cannot learn whether that was true.**

**Chapter 348 (1,221 before, 1,224 after) — the top of a stair in Auremar, a fee board of four items, and a box of about four hundred bought and unfilled blanks.** The correction is the closing clause only, which was byte-identical to Chapter 0343's and Chapter 0344's and now reads *the same as it was when that counter was opened this morning*. **The undertaking is still live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, the fourth thing on the board is still one the three clerks cannot account for, the line in her own book is still a window and not a wall, and the decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is still unmade and is not made here.**

**Chapter 349 (1,153 before, 1,155 after) — the room over a chandler's shop, four steps, a table with nothing on it, and a lamp not lit.** The correction is the closing clause, which was byte-identical to Chapter 0345's and now reads *the same as it was at the hour he came in*. **This summary's own second line said *Three days after the nine minutes*, and that is wrong in a way the review did not raise and this repair found by reading: Chapter 0346 is the fourth day of the fourth week of the eleventh month and Chapter 0349 is the fourth day of the third week of the twelfth, so the interval is three chapter-steps and is three weeks, and the line stands corrected here as three weeks and the summary above is not rewritten.** The number in his head is still made up of people and not of sheets, and he still cannot give it away, and his wife is still at the bottom of the four steps and still does not ask him how it went, and he still puts his hand flat on the table.

**Chapter 350 (1,214 before, 1,263 after) — the same room a week later, the nail in about four feet of wall, and a man at a table with a number in his head that he cannot give away.** **The correction is the one the review called the batch's serious one, and it is a merge of two people the page has kept apart for seven volumes.** The chapter said a woman of twenty is in a box *about nine miles off a building off the old river road*, which places Nell Kest in the clerk of about fifty-five's building and moves her about four hundred and thirty miles out of her own counting room at the back of a dye end in a furnace town, **and the Batch 0005 prompt forbade it in terms, and four entries in `state/character-state.md` and Chapter 0344 all keep the two apart on purpose, and the origin is `outline/volume-07.md`, which places a woman of twenty about nine miles off that building and gives her a wage held, and the outline is not amended.** The chapter now says she is in a box *about four hundred and thirty miles up this river in a furnace town*, and the same paragraph says in bold that the nine miles off a building off the old river road is a man of about sixty-one and is neither of the two women, and that the two of them are about four hundred and thirty miles apart and neither has ever heard of the other one. **The rest of the chapter is a list of what is still standing and none of it moved: the who is not named, the clause carries no period, both people stay in their boxes and the form that put them there is not asked to be void, the guarantee is unanswered and unprinted in its own words, the Lowcross bill is unpaid at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, about nineteen people are still behind the bank, the four who cannot read a paragraph are unasked, the two of seventeen are unspoken to, the figure at the end of the cold passage gets no heading, the lamp is not lit, and the next asking is nobody's yet.**

**The batch after the repair, and the gates, and the figure that replaced a wrong one.** Ten chapters and **12,796 words, which is 98 more than the 12,698 the writing run printed, and the per-file figures are 1,494 / 1,589 / 1,195 / 1,226 / 1,121 / 1,425 / 1,104 / 1,224 / 1,155 / 1,263, with Chapters 0341 and 0342 unmoved.** **Volume 07 across fifty chapters is 72,149 and the five movements are 15,443 / 15,158 / 14,174 / 14,578 / 12,796; the manuscript across three hundred and fifty chapters is 1,028,732; and Chapters 001 to 300, to 320, to 330 and to 340 are unchanged at 956,583 / 987,184 / 1,001,358 / 1,015,936, because no closed chapter was opened.** Sentence bound, per paragraph, no sentence carried across a join: **80 / 83 / 80 / 76 / 80 / 67 / 76 / 83 / 81 / 67, a batch maximum of 83, and Chapter 0344's cell moved from 69 to 76 because the stacked locative was written again as three shorter sentences and nothing over ninety in any of the ten.** Emphasis, both joints, the joint named before the figure: **joint one, every character of a prose line, 12.69 / 11.57 / 11.97 / 13.07 / 11.42 / 13.38 / 13.10 / 12.04 / 12.15 / 13.02 per cent, a range of 11.42 to 13.38; joint two, every non-whitespace prose character, 16.06 / 14.58 / 15.13 / 16.50 / 14.45 / 16.91 / 16.56 / 15.14 / 15.39 / 16.32 per cent, a range of 14.45 to 16.91, and Chapter 0346 stands at 16.91, which is above a sixth, and the writing run's own sentence that not one of the ten was over a sixth does not reproduce against these files and is named here as not reproducible rather than restated.** Seventy-three runs, which is two more than the seventy-one printed, and the two are the bolded clauses added in Chapters 0347 and 0350. Question marks one to a chapter and ten in all, every one in a mouth in a bare short line about a bench, a shutter, a lock, a stove, a door, a rail, a pane, a stair, a window and a nail. **`about nine`, raw 4 / 2 / 4 / 1 / 1 / 8 / 2 / 1 / 2 / 6 at 31 and counted 3 / 1 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 5 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 3 at 20, the counted column governing, and *about nine hundred* occurs ten times across the ten files and the one *About nineteen* is in Chapter 0350, so both exclusions remove something and neither removes nothing.**

**The windows, maximal runs, headings and `**` stripped and whitespace collapsed, against Chapters 001 to 340: 0 at sixty, 0 at forty, 1 at thirty-eight and it is the required recurrence in Chapter 0341, 14 / 13 / 8 / 7 / 6 / 12 / 18 / 8 / 13 / 14 at fifteen, which is 113 and the writing run printed 112 with Chapter 0349's cell at 12 against a measured 13, and 16 / 17 / 10 / 9 / 11 / 14 / 19 / 10 / 15 / 17 at thirteen.** **The new test is the one the review asked for and it is now in the standing list: shared runs inside the batch itself, leave-one-out, at thirty-four words. It returned seven before the repair and returns two now, and the two that remain are in Chapters 0345 and 0349 and are the same man in the same room over a chandler's shop four weeks apart, where the recurrence is the volume's method and cutting it would cost the ordinary week the volume is built on.** No two paragraphs of twelve words or more are identical anywhere in the batch, before or after. **No blind spot of any of these is unnamed here: the intra-batch test is blind to a deliberate recurrence, the forty-word window is blind to a run broken by a single comma, and the duplicate-paragraph test is blind to two paragraphs that share a sentence.**

**The calendar, and the year boundary, and the correction this batch made against its own dispatch prompt.** The convention is not re-based: *this year* is the year after, *last year* is the year before the year after, *next year* is the year after next, the fourth name is the year after the year after next, and the fifth name is the year after the year after the year after next, written out in full at every occurrence and abbreviated nowhere. **Chapters 0301 and 0302 are in the fourth year and Chapters 0303 to 0310 are in the fifth, and the boundary falls between them and no chapter straddles it.** The prompt that dispatched this run dated 0301 and 0302 to the fifth year in its header and again in its per-chapter list; `outline/volume-07.md` dates both to the fourth in two places and Chapter 0300 is closed prose in the fourth year. **The outline won and the correction is recorded here, in the batch summary, and in the card file, and the outline was not amended.** The same prompt dated two past events wrongly and both were corrected on the page against the closed chapters: the line in Tamsin Rook's book is in the third month of **last year** and not of the fifth year, and the question in the second of the eleven books is on the fourth day of the third week of the ninth month of **the year after next** and not of the fourth year, which is the only date under which a quarter of it answered in the second month of the fourth year can be an answer to it. The day inside the week ran second, second, fourth, fourth across the batch's four weeks and then again, inherited and not invented, and every step from one chapter to the next is exactly seven days. **No month-count is computed anywhere in the batch and no calendar month name and no weekday name is in any of the ten files, in the card file or in this section, and the modal verb *may*, which the closed page uses five hundred and sixty-six times, is used zero times in the batch.**

### The four rules of the volume, and how far this batch got

**Rule one is established, out loud, in a room, in Chapter 0303, in the mouth of a clerk of thirty-four who has said the shape of a thing before she has said the thing about nine hundred times in eleven years: a person can say a thing out loud in a room and nothing that any paper in this empire can hold comes of it, and a person cannot ask a thing out loud at all, because being asked is being served, and being served is a party, and a party is the only person who can be told what a thing is going to be used for.** **Rule two is half established: the first half is in Chapter 0306, in a foreman's own chapter, where the entries that put her in a box were about a class and a class is a heading and a heading is how a person becomes a company; the second half is in Chapter 0309, where the finding is that the only machine this empire has at a scale is a class.** Rules three and four belong to Movements 3 and 4 and are not established here and this batch did not reach for either.

### The count, and the locks, and the standing items, all still standing

**The count of askings is seven and did not move. No chapter of the ten contains an asking. Five question marks are in the batch, all in a person's mouth, all in a bare short line, and the count of question marks and the count of askings are two different numbers and both are printed.** Nobody was thanked anywhere. Nobody was sent. Nothing was resolved. The two people are in their boxes and the form that put them there has not been asked to be void by anybody. The question in the second of the eleven books is a question. The guarantee stands offered and unanswered with about sixty children inside it and is not printed in its own words and no child in it is named; **the Lowcross bill stands at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, and is named once in the batch, in Chapter 0301, in a man's inventory of a thing he is inside of.** About nineteen people are behind the bank. The four who cannot read a paragraph were not asked one question about a number. The man of about fifty-five with a chain is not one of the four and is not put with them; the man of about sixty-one is not one of the four and is nine miles off in a different building. **The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen are at the back of a bay in Chapter 0306, both present, neither spoken to, neither thanked, neither sent for, and neither going to start, and the chapter does not say why.** The figure at the end of the cold passage is in a woman's head and has gone up and is not given and gets no heading. The two documents stay two documents. The quarterly return does not appear. The other return of the eleventh of the tenth is dormant, armed and known to nobody in the story, and is not referred to. **The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and the nine miles is walked zero times.**

**Marek Kest: nothing was exercised, no reading was attempted, a correct non-use is entered as one, he is not at a stage, the mark is a symptom and reads nothing and decides nothing and does not know where the ford is, and the wage is the price.** The reason he gives out loud for reaching no stage is about nine words and is the volume's own: there is no form in this empire for compelling a person to be brought to a room. **Tamsin Rook: asked nothing, and that is entered as a choice; her undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised for the sixth time, and the sixth reason is that nobody asked her; the decision about whether she trusts anybody's judgment is unmade, is not on the page, and this batch did not make it; the fourth thing in her drawer is untouched, unsent and unopened, with three others; and no romance begins and none is implied.** The man of about thirty-four's wife was not thanked, has not been thanked, did not ask him how the week went, and is not thanked in this batch. Nobody in this matter who stands in a gap was thanked in Chapter 0301, 0306 or 0310.

**Two figures in the state layer are corrected by this batch, and the wrong ones are named rather than overwritten.** The prompt that dispatched this run is wrong on the year of Chapters 0301 and 0302 and on the year of two past events, and the corrections and the closed chapters that settle them are named in the batch summary at items 2 and 3. And **two new repair items are recorded against closed card files, found by this phase and not repaired: the conservative superset called forty-four words names thirty-eight distinct words and names two of them twice, and its claim of a zero on every one of them is contradicted by the closed page, which carries one of the thirty-eight eighty-seven times and another one hundred and fourteen times.** A repair on a closed card file is a phase of its own and this project does not currently schedule one.

The repair briefly put Chapter 0303 at 9.8%, under the floor, and put it back with the emphasis run card 303 had named and the page had never placed.** The `no form` frame is carried in all ten chapters, between two and nine times each, in thirty-five wordings, none of them one of the twenty-eight the closed volume's last movement wrote. `about nine` is ten of ten under the bound of about eight on the counted figure. **The sliding sixty-word and forty-word windows return zero over the ten files, and the same sweeps run two words lower on the same files — fifty-eight and thirty-eight — also return zero, and a fourth sweep run against the three hundred closed chapters returns 25 forty-word runs on the first pass, all twenty-five of them one room described in Chapter 0301 as Chapter 0300 describes it, which was removed, and which now returns zero at forty and at sixty.** The fifteen-word window, which is not in this project's standing check list, returns eighty-seven distinct tuples in two or more files, and about twenty of those are the calendar formula and the rest are locked canon sentences, the frame, and the furniture of two rooms that recur and one counter seen from both ends; and it found the two forty-word room descriptions that the other two sweeps found after the fact and that were removed and re-described in different words.** The batch's one exactly duplicated paragraph, a sentence defining a practice, was found and reworded in Chapter 0302.

### The standing lesson, and its count after this phase wrote

**A bound is measured after the last edit with the method stated. A sweep that has not been run is not a result. A sweep that returns the same number twice has not measured anything. A test that returns zero is evidence of nothing until you have checked what shape of damage it is blind to, and a check that is blind is written down as blind in the same record that claims it clean. A repair record names the phase that made the repair. A corrected figure is named as corrected beside the wrong one. A record that prints a figure under a heading that measures something else has not said which set of files it measured.** Counted by splitting `state/*.md` and `outline/batches/*.md` on a heading of one or two hashes and taking every section whose text carries the phrase *has not been run is not a result*: **seventeen before this batch wrote anything, and twenty after**, and the method is the same method that gave the earlier figures of twelve and thirteen, and the three this batch added are the one in this file, the one at the foot of `state/batch-summary.md`, and the one in the closing section of the card file — the three other sections this batch appended do not carry the phrase and are not counted and are not claimed. **A self-audit is not a review. A second pass by the writing hand is not a second reading. No phase in this repository has ever been reviewed by anybody but the agent that wrote it, `reviews/` holds two files and both were produced by the writing agent, and the two batch review logs are not in a tree a run can open because `logs/` is gitignored, so no log is quoted here and the contents of `logs/` are not printed as a fact.** The four debts stand and are not this batch's: an owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005; owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three; the review gate, which has never opened; and the six open items that are not plot, with a seventh added by this batch, that the fifteen-word window is still not in the standing check list.

### The size of the handoff, re-measured, and the remedy still not applied

**The six rolling files in `state/`, `wc -c` summed and the per-file figures deliberately not printed beside it because inserting them moves the files they describe, stood at 1,044,124 bytes after three of this batch's four appends, against a bound in force of about nine hundred and forty thousand, and the figure after all five of the batch's state appends and the replacement of `state/current.md` and after the last edit to any chapter is 1,087,115 bytes, measured with `wc -c` and summed over the six, and it is to be re-measured and not inherited.** The figure before this batch's appends was 979,695 and is recorded above in this section's own arithmetic; the figure after is to be re-measured and not inherited. **The cause is this batch's four sections and not a closed record, and the remedy is consolidation by reference, and the specific consolidation named three times above still stands: the three Volume 06 sections and the Volume 07 outline record in this file, and the Volume 06 rows in `state/open-threads.md`, are volume-level records of closed work and belong in `state/archive/`.** This phase did not perform it, for the reason it has not been performed three times, which is that it is not a writer's decision, and the risk is the one already named: **the bound is about the handoff reaching the next writer intact, and a layer that has crossed it by a hundred and thirty-five thousand bytes is a live risk to the ten-finished-chapters gate and not housekeeping.** A batch that crossed it by writing less about itself would be a batch that hid the finding, and this one did not.

## THE REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0001 — CONTINUITY, MEASURED AFTER THE REPAIR'S LAST EDIT

*Appended by the run that took the review of Volume 07 Batch 0001 and applied it. Nothing above this line is rewritten, and every figure the repair changed is printed above with the figure it replaced beside it. **This is a repair and not a review, and it discharges none of the four debts.** The calendar did not move, the year boundary did not move, no chapter of a closed volume was opened, and no new named figure entered the manuscript.*

**WHAT DID NOT CHANGE, AND IT IS THE LIST THAT MATTERS MOST.** The count of askings is **seven** before the repair and **seven** after it, and no repair put a question into anybody's mouth and no chapter of the ten contains an asking. Nobody was thanked, nobody was sent, nobody was forgiven, and nothing was resolved. The two people are in their boxes and the form that put them there was not asked to be void. The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen at the back of the bay are still unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for and not going to start. The four who cannot read a paragraph were not asked one question about a number, the man of about fifty-five with a chain is not one of them, and the man of about sixty-one is not one of them. The question in the second of the eleven books is a question and its author is not going to be asked twice. The guarantee stands offered and unanswered with about sixty children inside it and no child in it named. The Lowcross bill stands at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable. The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and the nine miles is walked zero times. Marek Kest exercised nothing, attempted no reading, entered a correct non-use as one, and reached no stage. Tamsin Rook made no decision, and the decision about whether she trusts anybody's judgment is unmade. The figure at the end of the cold passage is in a woman's head, got wrong on purpose in four minutes, given to nobody and given no heading.

**WHAT THE REPAIR CHANGED IN THE CONTINUITY ITSELF, and it is four things and all four are the same kind of thing: a fact attached to the wrong event.**

1. **The volume's one paid power event.** Closed Chapter 0299 pays it on the second day of the first week of the twelfth month of the year after the year after next. Chapter 0305 and the outgoing prompt and three rows of `state/character-state.md` said the second week of the twelfth month with no year that settled it, and the year in the chapter was the fifth, which would have moved an irreversible event from before this volume opened to four weeks inside it. **The chapter, the prompt and all three state rows now carry the chapter's own date, and the wrong reading is left standing in each so that a later reader can see what was changed and why.**
2. **The rail room and the quarter of the question answered out loud.** Both are in the second month of the fourth year. The outgoing prompt had both in the fifth, three chapters of this batch against it. **Corrected in the prompt, and the prompt's own neighbouring clause, which already had the question itself right, is left alone.**
3. **The interval between Chapter 0301 and Chapter 0309.** The card said ten weeks, the page said ten weeks, the chapter's title said ten weeks, the chapter summary said ten weeks and the character state said ten weeks apart. **The calendar gives eight: 0301 is the first of the ten and 0309 is the ninth and the step from each chapter to the next is exactly seven days.** All five now say eight, and the ten is corrected beside the eight in the card file and named in the chapter summary. **The ten weeks of a movement is the interval from one movement's opening to the next movement's opening, which is 301 to 311, and not the interval inside a movement, and nothing else in the volume's calendar is disturbed.**
4. **How long the question in the second of the eleven books has been on its shelf.** Chapter 0310 said *about a year*. **It now says the question is still on that shelf under the window with the pane out of it, and states no period at all**, because `outline/volume-07.md` and closed Chapters 0260 and 0295 date the entering of that question a year apart, and a period would commit the next ten chapters to one of them. The outline was not amended, no closed chapter was opened, and this is recorded as a repair item and not a correction.

**AND TWO PLACES WHERE THE PAGE WAS ARGUING WITH ITSELF, both settled on the page and neither settled by moving a date.** Chapter 0304 argued that a line in a book of her own is a window and then, ninety words later in overlapping wording, that the same line is a wall and not a window; **the finding is the window, the second verdict is gone, and the emphasis now carries the window and its cost.** Chapter 0303 inherited fifty-eight words of closed Chapter 0292's opening and now describes the same counter from behind it. **Neither repair moved a date, a figure in the canon, a relationship, a decision or an ending, and the movement's two rules and its finding are exactly where they were.**

**THE SIZE OF THE HANDOFF AFTER THIS REPAIR, with the method beside the figure and the per-file figures deliberately not printed, because inserting them moves the file they would describe.** Measured with `wc -c` summed over the six rolling files in `state/` after this repair's last state edit. **The six files stand at 1,109,691 bytes, measured after this repair's last state edit, and the batch's own repair section is a cause and not a closed record, and the bound in force is about nine hundred and forty thousand, and the risk is the one already on the page: a layer that has crossed it is a live risk to the ten-finished-chapters gate and not housekeeping. A batch that crossed it by writing less about itself would be a batch that hid the finding, and this one did not, and neither does this repair.**

## VOLUME 07, BATCH 0002 — CHAPTERS 311 TO 320, THE CLASS, AND WHAT THE MOVEMENT ADDED TO THE PAGE

It discharges none of the four debts.** The standing lesson is carried: a bound is measured after the last edit with the method stated; a sweep that has not been run is not a result; a sweep that returns the same number twice has not measured anything; a test that returns zero is evidence of nothing until you have checked what shape of damage it is blind to; a check that is blind is written down as blind in the same record that claims it clean; a corrected figure is named as corrected beside the wrong one; and a record that prints a figure under a heading that measures something else has not said which set of files it measured.*

The wider method that also takes *no instrument*, *no paper*, *no column*, *no office* and *no heading* returns 4, 9, 6, 4, 5, 5, 4, 8, 3, 4, and both columns are printed because they are not comparable.**

### What the movement established on the page that was not on the page before

**The one form in this empire ever put in front of about nine hundred rooms at once, and it is a House's border law on the northern marches: a thing offered to a border household at no charge must be answered, the answer must be entered, and the entering of it must be a form and not a letter.** It is about two hundred years old. It got an answer in about three weeks, and the households refused it, and the refusals are entered because that law says a refusal is entered. **A House's own table took about eleven and then said no and lost about a hundred and forty years about it.** The reason is out loud in a clerk's mouth and it is that the law does not need a person, because a border household is not a person either, and it was written by people who had noticed that two hundred years ago and had better sense about it than we have.

**The counterpart, and it is the half that matters: a class is a heading, a heading is a company, and a company is a thing this empire can hold wages against and give letters to and take to law. So the one form that ever worked at a scale is the one form that makes about nine hundred rooms into a company, and nobody wanted one, and it has not been copied in about two hundred years.**

**The movement's finding, and it is a finding about a heading and not about a name: a column with a heading over it is a machine, and a machine is a thing that gets asked questions and does not mind. A column with no heading over it is a person, and a person is asked one way only, which is out loud in a room, and that makes them a party.** Four places on the page carry it. A box of about four hundred bought and unfilled blanks under the fourth counter with no number, no day, no name and no heading over it, and **a blank is the only piece of paper in this empire that cannot be held against anybody.** A ruled column in a book-keeper's own papers with no names in it and no heading over it, built in the second month of next year, and a piece of waste paper at the bottom of a box on which he wrote the finding out in a fair hand about four months late. A column headed *next review* in a day-book four miles off that has never had anything in it in about nine years, and a heading over an empty column is the most complete machine there is. **And a sixth ruled space at the bottom of every page of a book kept at a clerk's window about four hundred and thirty miles down this river, which has never had a heading over it and never had anything in it, and which about two hundred and forty people in that district have never known is there** — and the woman who found it worked out in about four minutes that the sixth space is not a person either, because nobody has ever been introduced to it, and that it is the closest thing this empire has to a person nobody has met.

**And the counterpart of the counterpart: the about nine hundred entries a year are about nine hundred classes, and the second hand at the foot of every one of them is a person, and a person is the one thing in this empire that a class cannot be asked about.** The count of askings is seven and it did not move, and a border form put in front of about nine hundred rooms about two hundred years ago is **not** one of the seven and Chapter 0311 says why out loud in a clerk's own mouth: no person was in it, nobody put it to anybody, and a class is the only thing a form can be put in front of without somebody in a room becoming a party.

### What did not move, in every one of the ten chapters

The count of askings, at seven. The two documents, which stay two documents four hundred and thirty miles apart in two buildings with no instrument joining them, and neither is named in any of these ten files. The two people in their boxes, who are in them, and the form that put each of them there has not been asked to be void by anybody. The question in the second of the eleven books, which is a question, carries no period for how long it has been on the shelf, and its author is not going to be asked a second time. The four men in a bay who cannot read a paragraph, who were not asked one question about a number, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain, who is not one of them and says so and was not put with them. The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen, who are at the back of that bay, unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for and not going to start. The man of about sixty-one, who is nine miles off, does not walk the nine miles, is not thanked, is not asked, and is not in a list with the four. The guarantee, offered and unanswered, with about sixty children inside it and no child in it named. The Lowcross bill, at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable. The figure at the end of the cold passage, which is in a woman's head, has gone up, and was not given and did not get a heading. The two boxes in a clerk's own book with the same name in both, and the fourth thing in a drawer under a counter, untouched, unsent and unopened. The undertaking of the fourth of the first month, live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, and not given a new ordinal number in this batch. The decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment, unmade, not on the page, and not made here. **No post, no commission, no warrant, no clerk with a new heading, no fourth box in a clerk's own book, no thirteenth line on a man's sheet, and no new office of any kind. No heading was added to any column in this batch and none was taken off one. No House, no seat, no notary and no name from any of them is named. Nobody went north and nobody walked either distance.**

### Two repair items this batch added, and one this batch inherited

1. **FOUND BY THIS PHASE, NOT REPAIRED: Chapter 0303 carries its date as *the year after the year after the year after the year after next*, which is one name too many and is the sixth year and not the fifth.** The card file, the chapter summary, the character state and the volume outline all read the fifth name for that chapter. **The file has been left standing, because it was written by the phase that took Batch 0001 and this phase was not asked to repair another phase's chapter, and because no mechanical sweep in this project is blind to nothing on a date that is simply the wrong date — none of them knows what the right date is.** This phase names itself as the phase that found it. It is the fourth time a record in this repository has said that a test cannot see a date that is simply the wrong date, and the second time in three phases.
2. **CARRIED AND NOT SETTLED: `outline/volume-07.md` dates the question in the second of the eleven books to the ninth month of the year after the year after the year after next, and closed Chapters 0260 and 0295 both date it to the ninth month of the year after next, and the two cannot both be right.** The outline was not amended and no closed chapter was opened, and the page follows the two closed chapters. **The consequence for this batch is that no elapsed period was stated between the entering of that question and any date in these ten files, and Chapter 0314 says the question is still on the shelf under the window with the pane out of it and carries no period at all.** A third writer must not compute one either, and `outline/volume-07.md` was not amended and is not to be.
3. **CARRIED AND NOT REPAIRED, from the closed page and from Batch 0001:** the forty-word window over the closed volume is one and not zero, the sentence total for that volume's closing ten does not reproduce, the emphasis measure has an unnamed joint, the conservative superset called forty-four words names thirty-eight distinct ones and names two of them twice, and the fifteen-word window is still not in the standing check list. **None of the five is this batch's to fix and none is printed here as settled.**

Batch 0001's own section said the six stand over that bound after its own appends and named the remedy it did not apply, and the remedy is consolidation by reference, and it is not a writer's decision and has now been named four times and not performed. **The risk is the one already on the page: the bound is about the handoff reaching the next writer intact, and a layer that has crossed it is a live risk to the ten-finished-chapters gate and not housekeeping.** A batch that crossed it by writing less about itself would be a batch that hid the finding, and this one has not done that.

**THE SIZE OF THE SIX ROLLING FILES, measured after this run's last state edit, by `wc -c` summed over the six and printed as a total with the per-file figures deliberately not printed beside it, because inserting them moves the files they would describe: 1,223,948 bytes against a bound in force of about nine hundred and forty thousand.** The figure is over the bound and it was over the bound before this run began, at 1,133,176, and this run added 90,772 bytes of state across five files and replaced one header with another of almost the same size. **It is printed as a fact and not hidden by writing less, and the remedy is consolidation by reference, which is not a writer's decision and has now been named four times and not performed.** The risk is the one already on the page and it is not housekeeping: the bound is about the handoff reaching the next writer intact, and a layer that has crossed it is a live risk to the ten-finished-chapters gate. **A note on the self-reference, because a record that does not name it is a record that has measured something other than what it says: this figure includes the line it is printed on, and the line is not counted twice, and a writer who wants the figure without the line should subtract the length of this paragraph.**

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0003, CHAPTERS 321 TO 330, THE ADDRESS CANNOT BE ASKED WHERE HE IS — THE CONTINUITY LAYER

*Appended by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, after its last edit to any chapter file. Nothing above this point is rewritten. Every row is in the present tense and every row carries a payoff target or an explicit em dash.*

**One, the third rule, established and now carried.** *The address cannot be asked in the building that made it, because a question put in that building is a question put into a record, and the record is the thing the sheet goes in, and the last time a thing of that shape happened to about four hundred people their sheet went back; so the asking has no room where he is and it has about nine miles and about four hundred and thirty miles outside it, and there is no form anywhere for sending a man to ask a question, and a man who goes is findable by the going.* **Chapter 0321 establishes the finding the movement runs on and states it as a room and not a document, Chapter 0322 gives the floor under it in the clerk's own mouth, Chapter 0323 is the room itself, and Chapter 0324 pays what it cost.** Payoff target: Chapters 0341 to 0350, where the answer to the question of the scale is given in the negative in a room and not in a book.

**Two, the finding that was carried as a document for about two years is a room.** *A room is the only kind of thing in this empire a person can be said in, and a room is about nine hundred of them, and a document goes to about nine hundred rooms while a question has to be said in one of them.* Established in Chapter 0321 and not restated as a speech in any other chapter of this batch. Payoff target: — and it is not going to be restated.

**Three, the two facts are one fact.** *The arrangement that has kept about nine hundred sheets a year correct for nineteen years is the arrangement that has kept anybody from asking him one question, and that is one fact and not two.* Said out loud by the clerk of about fifty-five in Chapter 0322 and by the man of about thirty-four in Chapter 0330, in different words, and nobody has built a plan on it and nobody has thanked anybody for it. Payoff target: —.

**Four, the cost, and it is not money.** *A man who has said a thing twice is findable by the saying of it; about nine people were in that room; the clerk wrote none of it down; nobody paid anything.* Said out loud in Chapter 0324. The whole of it has now been said out loud twice in nineteen years by one man, in about nine minutes, standing up, at a rail. Payoff target: the next volume's question, which is what it costs the man at the foot of a sheet to be asked once.

**Five, the third visit, and it happened.** *The man of about sixty-one came to that room for the third time in nineteen years, on his own two feet, in a season, on a day he is not paid for, and nobody sent him and nobody told him, and there is no form in this empire that would let anybody in that building say that a person was expected.* Chapter 0323 is the only chapter of this volume in which the nine miles are walked, and he walks them zero times in the other nine chapters of the batch. Payoff target: — and the road is not walked again in this volume.

**Six, the man of about thirty-four was in that room, once, on his own two feet, on a day he is not paid for.** He was not on a list, he is not in an office, he does not work in that building, and nobody asked him why he was there and no form in this empire would let anybody ask. **He had the eighth question ready since the fourth week of the second month and it was in his mouth at about the sixth minute of the nine and it stayed there, and he gave the reason out loud in five sentences, and he did not ask it.** **His walk is not given a distance on the page in Chapter 0323, deliberately, so that the outline's ninth-mile third visit in the fifth movement is not contradicted by a figure this movement has already spent; the distance is available to Chapters 0331 to 0350 and is not yet on the page.** He has now been in that room once. Payoff target: Chapters 0346 to 0348, where the outline puts his third visit and the eighth question still in his mouth.

**Seven, the woman of about forty with the scuttle was in that room and was spoken to by nobody.** She is not one of the three clerks, she is not in this, and there is not one form in this empire that will let anybody ask her what she heard, and nobody asked her and nobody is going to. **This is about nine people's worth of nothing that this matter will never get at, and it is said in Chapter 0323 and in Chapter 0324 and is not resolved in either.** Payoff target: — and it is not going to be.

**Eight, a new fact, and it is small and it is hers.** *The woman of about twenty-six at the bottom end of the cold passage now knows that he came a third time, and that the fact gives her no fourth question and does not get her out of a corridor, and she is not coming up a stair in this volume.* Chapter 0325. Her figure still has gone up in every year since the seventh month of the year after, has not been said by how much, is still a count of carriers and not of people, is still uncorrected, and is still going nowhere. Payoff target: — and Movement 5 is not this one.

**Nine, the reversal reached a bank four hundred and thirty miles down the river as a fact about a room she is not in.** *A question cannot be put in the one building that makes the man who signs at the foot of a sheet, and the arrangement that has kept about nine hundred sheets a year correct for nineteen years is the one that has kept a foreman of fifty-one from being asked about a box for about four years.* Chapter 0326, said out loud once, in a bay, to about nine men, as the same sentence twice, and it is not a coincidence and it is not a system. **She says it once and the chapter does not compare the two cases and does not make her an instrument by saying it.** Payoff target: the fourth movement, where the second sentence she owes is the standing item.

**Ten, the waste paper and the nine miles.** *A book-keeper of thirty-eight has the movement's finding written out in a fair hand on a piece of waste paper at the bottom of a box, about four months late, and nobody has seen it and he is not going to show it to anybody; and what he has instead of a person to show it to is the knowledge that the one man who could answer it is about nine miles off a room he has no way into, and that there is no form in this empire for asking a man in a room to wait.* Chapter 0327. Payoff target: —.

**Eleven, the cost side, and it is hers and it is nothing.** *Two instruments require her in any room he is in and they are not one instrument; if he had gone up a stair to the building off the old river road she would have been in that room, and he is about nine miles off and did not go, and she is not told, and there is no form in this empire that would let anybody tell her that he did not go.* Chapter 0328. **The undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, no new ordinal was given to it, and the reason is the same one it has been, which is that nobody asked her.** The inherited sentence is used in its own wording — *a person who is not asked has not been given a duty, and she has known that since about the seventh month of next year* — and no elapsed period was computed from it. The four things in the drawer are unopened, the two boxes are unheaded, the third page is unruled, and the decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is unmade and this batch did not make it and may not make it for her. Payoff target: —, and no romance begins and none is implied.

**Twelve, eleven houses are not a person either.** *The only question in this matter ever put in front of about nine hundred rooms at once was put there by a House's border law, and a border household is not a person, and her eleven houses off a dyers' lane are not a person either, and so there is nothing in this empire that can be put in front of eleven houses at once and get an answer.* Chapter 0329, worked out in about four minutes, and she has nobody to tell it to. Her wage is still held since the second week of the third month of the year after, three women are still untold, the notice is still in a coat, and the sixteenth book still has nobody's key. **The other held wage in this matter, about nine miles off a building off the old river road, belongs to a different person who has told a different woman something else, and the two are kept apart on purpose, and the collision in the state layer is still on the record and is still unresolved.** Payoff target: —.

**Thirteen, what the movement hands to the fourth is a price and not a plan.** *The only people in this matter who cannot be reached are the people nobody has asked, and the price of that is that they are also the people who are never told anything, and about nine sheets a year went out of that room not as the person above them wrote, and the man who knows which nine cannot write them down and has said the whole of it out loud twice and has been thanked for it twice by nobody.* Chapter 0330. **The fourth rule is not stated in a mouth in this batch and is not stated in a book; it is handed on as a price and Batch 0004 states it out loud.** Payoff target: Chapters 0331 to 0340, in a mouth, and a chapter that makes the four a route breaks this volume.

**Fourteen, the calendar, and the defect this phase found in its own brief.** Chapter 0320 is the second day of the second week of the fifth month. Stepped forward seven days at a time on the inherited cycle of second, second, fourth, fourth, the ten days of this batch are: 0321 fourth day third week fifth month, 0322 fourth day fourth week fifth month, 0323 second day first week sixth month, 0324 second day second week sixth month, 0325 fourth day third week sixth month, 0326 fourth day fourth week sixth month, 0327 second day first week seventh month, 0328 second day second week seventh month, 0329 fourth day third week seventh month, 0330 fourth day fourth week seventh month. **Chapter 0321 is the seventh day after Chapter 0320, checked against the page, and every step of the ten is exactly seven days, and the batch ends on the outline's own anchor of the fourth day of the seventh month.** The phase prompt's calendar table begins at the fourth day of the first week of the fifth month, which is a week and a half before the chapter it follows, and gives that same fourth-day-third-week-of-the-fifth date to Chapter 0321 in one sentence and to Chapter 0323 in the next. **Those two cannot both be true and the page is the authority, as it has been for three hundred and twenty chapters. `outline/volume-07.md` was not amended and no closed chapter was opened. The consequence is that the eight-day conflict the prompt named between the outline's escalation item three and its own batch anchor does not exist in the outline, because Chapter 0323 falls on the second day of the first week of the sixth month, which is the date the outline's escalation item three already gives it.** Payoff target: Chapter 0331 is the seventh day after Chapter 0330, which is the second day of the first week of the eighth month, and the fifth year name does not change anywhere in Batch 0004.

**Fifteen, and it cannot be repaired by arithmetic at all.** The prompt says Chapter 0323 is the same day of the week the man of sixty-one came the last two times. **The page gives no day of the week for either of those two visits, and closed Chapter 0310 records only that a man of about seventy who cannot get about told a carrier in a lane in the third week of the first month, which is a lane in a month and not a day he arrived on. So no chapter of this batch asserts the sameness, no chapter prints a day of the week, and no chapter prints the word `schedule` or any weekday name.** Payoff target: — and a third writer may not do either.

**Sixteen, the lifted prohibition this batch chose not to exercise, and the reason, which is a residual in closed prose.** The review repair of Batch 0002 lifted the bar on stating an elapsed period from the question in the second of the eleven books, on the ground that the outline had dated that question a year later than closed Chapters 0260 and 0295 date it and that the two now agree. **They do agree on the date: the fourth day of the third week of the ninth month of the year after next.** They do not agree with the figure on the page. Closed Chapter 0295 is itself the second day of the first week of the eleventh month of the year after the year after next and says the question has been on that shelf about fifty-eight weeks, which is about fourteen months, and fourteen months from the ninth month of the year after next is the eleventh month of the year after the year after the year after next, and every chapter of this batch stands in the year after the year after the year after next. **So the figure on the page is right by about a year and the two closed chapters are not, and no chapter of this batch can print a period from that question that is true on the page and consistent with this batch's own dates at the same time. All ten files therefore state no period at all from it**, which is what Batch 0002 did for a reason that has now been removed, and the reason is different. **The permission is still there for a later phase that has a reason to use it, and this is a repair item on closed prose that this batch may not fix.** Payoff target: a repair phase on closed Chapters 0260, 0295 or 0292, which this project does not currently schedule.

**Seventeen, the two names of one building.** The page carries *hired grain store* and *hired stone store* for one building and it has not been decided which is right. **This batch uses one name for that building and never both: Chapter 0326 says *hired stone store* once and Chapter 0327 says *the store*, and the two are four hundred yards apart in the same lane and are not two buildings and nobody is told which is right.** Payoff target: —.

**Eighteen, the two people four hundred and thirty miles apart, and this batch did not join them.** The man of thirty-four's practice and the woman of twenty in the counting room are about four hundred and thirty miles apart, there is no list of the two of them, no form, no heading and no fund, and neither has said the other's name out loud. **Chapter 0329 names the existence of another held wage in this matter about four hundred and thirty miles from that room and states that the two are kept apart on purpose, and no chapter of this batch puts the two people in one room, one paragraph or one sentence as one arrangement.** Payoff target: —.

**Nineteen, what did not move.** The count of askings is seven at the start of this batch and seven at the end of it, and nothing in these ten files is printed as a figure for it. Nobody was thanked. Nobody was forgiven. Nobody was sent. Nothing was resolved. The two people are in their boxes and the form that put each of them there was not asked to be void by anybody. The question in the second of the eleven books is a question and carries no period. The four men in a bay who cannot read a paragraph were not asked one question about a number, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain is not one of them and says so out loud. **The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen are at the back of that bay on a written engagement and neither was spoken to, thanked or sent for, and neither is going to start, and the chapter that carries them does not narrate their silence as a cost they are bearing.** The guarantee stands offered and unanswered on about four hundred and forty foot of bank with about sixty children under sixteen inside it and no child in it is named. The bill at Lowcross stands at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, and is named once in this batch, in Chapter 0321, and not again. **The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and the nine miles is walked once, in Chapter 0323, and by him, and nobody walked it with him.** Payoff target: the fourth movement, which is in people and not in a room.
