# VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — CHAPTERS 0331 TO 0340, THE FOURTH MOVEMENT, AND WHAT IT PUT ON THE PAGE

**This section was written by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. It is a closing record and not a review, and nothing above this point in this file is rewritten.**

## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — TWO CONTINUITIES THAT WERE FALSE, THE FIGURES THAT MOVED, AND WHAT A REPAIR IS NOT

**Appended downward and nothing above this line is edited. The line this section corrects by name is `state/continuity.md` line 826, which reads *a man with a fair hand four hundred yards up the same lane who wants the rest of a half-sentence*, and it is wrong about the man and right about everything else: the creditor is Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, who keeps the books, and the man of about thirty-four with the fair hand has no part in that sentence and never had. The same correction is made in the chapters and in `state/character-state.md`, and this file's earlier line is left standing because this file is append-only and a correction that overwrites the line it corrects is a correction that destroys the evidence that the error was ever on the page.**

**One, a debt given to two men, and the fact that nothing in the battery can see that class of thing.** Chapter 0331 put into the mouth of the man of about thirty-four a want for the rest of a foreman's sentence that the page gives to the book-keeper four hundred yards up the same lane, and Chapter 0332 gave the same debt, in the same words and from the same month, to the right man, and then repeated the wrong one four paragraphs later by calling him *a man with a fair hand*. **All three are corrected. The reason it survived is the finding: the two men are both on the page, both are described by a fair hand, the ages are thirty-four or thereabouts and thirty-eight, neither is named where the debt is spoken of, and the distance given in the wrong sentence matched neither man's position. A check that counts figures, windows, question marks and emphasis runs has nothing to say about who holds a debt, and a batch that only runs the battery will keep making this mistake.** Standing rule: **name the holder of a debt by the thing that distinguishes him and not by his trade, and check the debt against the ledger before the chapter is closed.**

**Two, a visit figure the outline asserts and the page contradicts, and which of the two governs.** `outline/volume-07.md` gives the man of about thirty-four the **third** time in the rail room at the climax, at two separate places, one of them the ninth-mile line. **Chapter 0323 gives him the first**, in two separate places: *a man of about thirty-four who had not been in the room before*, and *he has been in a room at the top of four flights of a stair in a town about four miles off, twice, and he paid fourpence for a copy both times, and neither of those rooms is this one*. **And this file's own line 780, written by Batch 0003, says *the man of about thirty-four was in that room, once*. Three sources against one, and the one is an outline and the three are the page and the record of the page, so the page governs and the figure is the second.** Chapter 0340 is corrected; the phrase *the two thirds are not one third* is struck from it; the Batch 0005 prompt is corrected in three places; **the outline is not amended, because a repair phase has no authority over the volume outline of record, and the defect is recorded as open item 20 against `state/open-threads.md`, which is the same standing as item 15, where the defect was in a prompt and not in the outline.** A second figure for the same beat was settled with it: the reason-speech is *about nine sentences* in the outline and in the prompt, and Chapter 0340 said *about four minutes before it*, and Chapter 0323's five sentences were a different speech given afterwards and outside the room, so the three are now two and the chapter takes the outline's nine.

**Three, a contradiction inside one chapter, found in passing and not by the review.** Chapter 0340 said in one paragraph that a man of sixty-one *will be* in the room at the climax and in the next that *the two of them are not going to be in the room at the same time*. **Both cannot be true and the outline requires both men in the room, so the second sentence is the one that goes, and what replaces it says the thing that is true and is the point: they are in the room at the same time, only one of them has walked those nine miles this season, and nobody is in a position to arrange that either.** The Batch 0005 prompt already said this and said it better, and the chapter now agrees with the prompt.

**The figures that moved, and the whole of what moved them.** **Every figure Batch 0004's closing record printed went stale the moment the repair touched four sentences in four chapters, and each was re-measured after the last edit with the method named, and the old figure is printed beside the new in `state/current.md` rather than overwritten.** The batch total is 14,578 against 14,529; Chapters 001 to 340 are 1,015,936 against 1,015,887; the volume across forty chapters is 59,353 against 59,304; the four movements are 15,443 / 15,158 / 14,174 / 14,578. The six closed volumes and Chapters 001 to 330 are unchanged at 956,583 and 1,001,358, **because no closed chapter was opened and no volume outside this one was touched.** The sentence bound holds under both readings and the looser reading governs, batch maximum 80 in Chapter 0337, and the repair's own method reproduces the writing run's published looser column in nine cells of ten. Both emphasis columns moved, joint one to a range of 11.29 to 13.06 and joint two to 14.26 to 16.48, **and not one of the ten is over a sixth; the first two drafts of the repair put a fifty-five-word and a twenty-eight-word run into Chapters 0331 and 0340 and Chapter 0331 went to 17.98 per cent on joint two, and the cure was to shorten the run to a clause and to split one sentence in two, and not to delete anything.** The `about nine` columns moved to raw 47 and counted 34. The governing bounds did not move: **the sixty-word and forty-word windows are zero inside the ten files and against Chapters 001 to 330, under a maximal-run reading as well as a sliding-window one, and the duplicate-paragraph test is zero, and the count of askings is seven at the start, seven at the end and seven now, and the per-file question-mark count is still one in each of the ten.** The count of askings is the one figure in this matter a repair could most easily have moved and did not, and that is checked by hand and not by a test.

**And what this repair is, and is not, stated once.** It applied findings written by a second hand to a batch written by the first, which is a third act in a project that has never had a review. **The review gate opened on this batch and fell back to the writing agent again, so the findings were not independent and the repair discharges no debt: the four debts are unchanged, the owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005 is four volumes on, the four owed second readings are four, and a self-audit, a second pass, a repair and a review are four different things.** It rewrote no closed chapter, amended no outline, and edited no controller file: not `scripts/`, not `.github/workflows/`, not `.opencode/agent/`, not `AGENTS.md`, `PHASE_SYSTEM.md`, `REPO_PLAN.md`, `OUTLINE_GUIDE.md`, `opencode.json`, and not `state/phase-ledger.json`, **which still reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and is more than twenty phases out of date and remains a standing blocker that nothing in the phase system can clear.** It wrote no marker in its own directory. **It also did not consolidate the state layer, and the bound is still breached, and the figure is printed at the foot of `state/current.md` whatever it is: the standing remedy is consolidation by reference, it has now been named eight times, it is not a writer's decision, and a phase with authority over it has still not acted.**

# VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005, CHAPTERS 341 TO 350 — CONTINUITY AT THE FIFTH MOVEMENT, WITH THE MEASURED FIGURES AND THE DEBTS UNCHANGED

**This section is appended downward and nothing above it is rewritten, which is the standing rule for this file. Every figure in it was measured by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0005/PROMPT.md`, after that run's last edit to any chapter, per file, with the method named in the line it measures, and none was inherited. The full closing record, with every blind spot beside its result, is at the foot of `state/batch-summary.md` and is not repeated here.**

## THE REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — WHAT THE REVIEW CHANGED ON THE PAGE, AND WHAT IT DELIBERATELY DID NOT CHANGE

**Written downwards by the phase that reviewed Batch 0005 and repaired it. Nothing above this line is rewritten. Six prose faults were repaired in five chapters, one merge was repaired in a handoff, five figures in the writing run's closing record did not reproduce and are named as wrong, and one new bound was added to the standing list. No closed chapter was opened, no plan figure was edited to match a file, and no thread of the volume moved.**

**One, the merge, and it is the finding.** Chapter 0350 said a woman of twenty is in a box *about nine miles off a building off the old river road*. That building is the clerk of about fifty-five's, on a ground floor off that road with a shelf under a window about two feet wide, and the woman of twenty is in a counting room at the back of a dye end in a furnace town about four hundred and thirty miles up the river, and the two have been kept apart in the state layer since Volume 03, in four entries in `state/character-state.md` and in the Batch 0005 prompt itself, which forbade it in terms. **The chapter now places her about four hundred and thirty miles up this river in a furnace town and says, in the same paragraph and in bold, that the nine miles off a building off the old river road is a man of about sixty-one and is neither of the two women.** Chapter 0344's closing movement, which had stacked three distances into one clause and left the building unidentifiable, now names the building as the one off the old river road in a town down the river and names the woman in it as one who stands at the end of a cold passage. **The origin is `outline/volume-07.md`, whose fifth section places a woman of twenty about nine miles off that building and gives her a wage held, and the outline is not amended, which is the same standing as open items 15 and 20. Closed Chapter 0301 prints the merged line and is not reopened, so one file of the manuscript still carries the collision and it is recorded as open item 29 rather than tidied away.** The same run's `workspace/volume-07/volume-close/PROMPT.md` had carried the merge forward as settled canon and would have handed it to Volume 08; that bullet is corrected, three standing items are added to it, and the close is told what it must say about the separation.

**Two, the interval, and three figures existed for one number.** Chapter 0347 said the nine minutes were **four days** behind the clerk of about fifty-five. The batch's own card said **three days**. The card's own title for that chapter said *the same week*. The calendar is one chapter to a week and puts Chapter 0347 exactly one seven-day step after Chapter 0346 — fourth day, fourth week, eleventh month, to second day, first week, twelfth month — **so the page now says seven days, the card says seven days, and the card's title is *The Same Room, The Afternoon Of The Week After*.** The 4/4 to 2/1 day-name jump at a month boundary is this volume's inherited four-week cycle, is identical across all fifty chapters of Volume 07, and was not touched; the nine-sevens arithmetic the writing run printed is inherited, and the figure a reader can check off the page is the one that was wrong. **Chapter 0349's summary in `state/chapter-summaries.md` also carried a wrong interval, *three days after the nine minutes*, where the interval is three chapter-steps and is three weeks; that line is corrected in the repair section of that file and the entry above it is not rewritten, which is that file's standing.**

**Three, a chapter that contradicted itself in three lines.** Chapter 0347 said a man of about thirty-four was in the room at the far end of the passage *and she was in that room and she wrote none of it*, and said two lines later that she is not told that he was there. She cannot be present and untold in the same page, and the second statement is the one the whole volume is built on: a woman who said in the ninth month that he is not coming back, and who cannot learn whether that was true, and for whom no form exists that would let a clerk of an office be told either thing. **The first was the error. The paragraph now says she was in her own room at the top end of that passage at the time of it and wrote none of it, which is the volume's own phrase for not entering a minute, and the beat that follows is untouched.** No bound in the standing list can see this class of damage; it was found by reading two adjacent paragraphs.

**Four, the reason-speech's sentence count.** Chapter 0346 said the speech was *in about nine sentences*. The speech is nine quoted paragraphs carrying **thirteen** sentences, and Chapter 0346 is the one chapter of the batch where a reader is invited to count. **The page now says about thirteen sentences, and the card and the batch's own decision paragraph now say thirteen.** The nine came from `outline/volume-07.md` and out of Batch 0004's repair of Chapter 0340, **and closed Chapter 0340 still says the man is going to say it in about nine sentences, so the two figures now stand apart on the page, Chapter 0340 is not reopened, and open item 31 carries the divergence unrepaired.** The two are not reconciled here by picking one, because a close that reconciles them by picking one is a close that has decided something on the page's behalf.

**Five, five chapters and two closings.** Chapters 0343, 0344 and 0348 closed on one byte-identical thirty-four-word clause and Chapters 0345 and 0349 on another. **Each of the five now has its own wording of the same fact, and the asking-count did not move and the ten weeks did not move**: the same as it was when the first rack went down this morning, when the stove was let go out, when that counter was opened this morning, when the lamp was not lit, and at the hour he came in. A sixth and seventh chapter of the batch already varied it, and Chapter 0350 varies it again. **The bound that would have caught this is new: shared runs inside the batch against each other, leave-one-out, at thirty-four words. It returns two after the repair and seven before, and the two that remain are Chapters 0345 and 0349, which are the same man in the same room over a chandler's shop four weeks apart, where the repetition is the volume's method and the ordinary week is what the volume is built on. That exemption is printed with the bound, because a bound with an unstated exemption is a bound that will be reported as a breach and then quietly not run.** No two paragraphs of twelve words or more are identical anywhere in the batch.

**Six, two sentences that did not stand up.** Chapter 0345's date line said the lamp *is not lit until about the seventh hour and was not lit then*, asserting a past fact about an hour that has not arrived at the fifth hour; it now reads *is not lit and is not going to be lit until about the seventh hour*, which is what Chapters 0341, 0349 and 0350 say of the same lamp. Chapter 0344's stacked locative is the first item above.

**The figures, re-measured after the last edit to any chapter, with the method named before every one of them.** Words, per file, `sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//' file | wc -w`, summed one file at a time: **1,494 / 1,589 / 1,195 / 1,226 / 1,121 / 1,425 / 1,104 / 1,224 / 1,155 / 1,263, batch total 12,796**, against the writing run's printed 12,698, and the ninety-eight words are the whole of the repair. **Volume 07 across fifty chapters is 72,149 against 72,051; the five movements are 15,443 / 15,158 / 14,174 / 14,578 / 12,796; the manuscript across three hundred and fifty chapters is 1,028,732 against 1,028,634; and Chapters 001 to 300, to 320, to 330 and to 340 are unchanged at 956,583 / 987,184 / 1,001,358 / 1,015,936, because no closed chapter was opened.** Sentence bound, per paragraph, no sentence carried across a join, the looser reading governing: **80 / 83 / 80 / 76 / 80 / 67 / 76 / 83 / 81 / 67, batch maximum 83**, and Chapter 0344's cell moved from 69 to 76 because the stacked locative was written again as shorter sentences, and nothing over ninety and nothing at a hundred. Emphasis, both joints, the joint named before the figure: **joint one, every character of a prose line, 12.69 / 11.57 / 11.97 / 13.07 / 11.42 / 13.38 / 13.10 / 12.04 / 12.15 / 13.02 per cent, range 11.42 to 13.38; joint two, every non-whitespace prose character, 16.06 / 14.58 / 15.13 / 16.50 / 14.45 / 16.91 / 16.56 / 15.14 / 15.39 / 16.32 per cent, range 14.45 to 16.91.** Seventy-three runs against the writing run's seventy-one, and the two are the bolded clauses added in Chapters 0347 and 0350. Question marks one to a chapter and ten in all, every one in a mouth in a bare short line, and none put to a person about a person. Windows as maximal runs, headings and `**` stripped and whitespace collapsed, against Chapters 001 to 340: **0 at sixty, 0 at forty, 1 at thirty-eight and it is the required recurrence in Chapter 0341, 14 / 13 / 8 / 7 / 6 / 12 / 18 / 8 / 13 / 14 at fifteen at 113, and 16 / 17 / 10 / 9 / 11 / 14 / 19 / 10 / 15 / 17 at thirteen at 138.**

**`about nine`, three columns with the exclusions named beside each other, and three of the writing run's per-file figures did not reproduce.** Measured on the ten files after this repair: **raw 4 / 2 / 4 / 1 / 1 / 8 / 2 / 1 / 2 / 6 at 31; counted 3 / 1 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 5 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 3 at 20, the counted column governing, the bound of about eight met in every file and the highest counted figure five.** The writing run printed **raw 4 / 3 / 5 / 1 / 1 / 10 / 2 / 1 / 2 / 7 at 36 and counted 3 / 2 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 7 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 5 at 25**, and its Chapters 0342, 0343 and 0346 were each exactly one high in the raw column and its Chapters 0342, 0346 and 0350 were each exactly one high in the counted column, **and both printed columns are named here as wrong rather than quietly replaced.** Two of this repair's own changes moved the counts down, in Chapter 0346 and in Chapter 0350, and the corrections are named with the figures beside them. **The writing run also stated that the *about nine hundred* exclusion removed nothing because that string does not occur; it occurs ten times across the ten files, and the single *About nineteen* is in Chapter 0350, so both exclusions remove something and the counted column is twenty and not twenty-two.** The card's ten field-nine promises were 4 / 4 / 4 / 3 / 3 / 6 / 4 / 4 / 4 / 6, **all ten differ from the counted column and eight of the ten differ from the raw column, so the writing run's own statement that four of the ten promises were wrong reproduces under neither reading and is named as not reproducible.** No sentence of any of the ten files was cut or stretched to suit a number in a plan.

**A second reading of the same window, and a third, and what they cannot see.** A forty-word window with punctuation kept in the token is the governing reading and returns zero. **A forty-word window with punctuation normalised away returns one run of forty-nine words, in Chapter 0342 against closed Chapter 0333, and it is Rennick Adley's own column in the second of the Company's books stated in the same words in three chapters of this volume; it is named here and not hidden and it is not cut, because the same man saying the same thing about the same column in his own lane is the page and not a lift.** A thirty-eight-word window with punctuation normalised away returns three: the one in Chapter 0341 that the governing reading also returns, the Chapter 0342 run above, and one in Chapter 0347 against closed Chapter 0336, which is the same woman's own question on the same shelf in the same words with four words dropped between them. **The blind spot is named beside each result: the governing window is blind to a run broken by a single comma, and this volume's prose is built out of sentences that differ from one another by a comma, so a zero from the governing reading is a weaker statement than it looks.**

**The threads this repair did not move, restated because a close is where a debt is most likely to be quietly dropped.** The count of askings is seven and is printed in no file. The who is not named and the second half of the question is not asked. The two documents stay two documents. The two sealed pages stay shut with two refusals between them and no third. The bundle stays shut and unnamed. The substituted wording keeps its four ages and no chapter adds a fifth. The quarterly return is still in no reply. The counting-house mark is a mark. The ninety-second leaf is not named. The Venn ruling's schedule is referred to zero times and the word does not occur. The clause in the second of the eleven books carries no period and its author is not asked a second time. The figure at the end of the cold passage is in a woman's head and gets no heading. The guarantee stands offered and unanswered with about sixty children inside it and no child named. About nineteen people are still behind the bank. The Lowcross bill stands at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, named once in this volume. The four who cannot read a paragraph are unasked about a number, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain and the man of about sixty-one are neither of them one of them. The two of seventeen are unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, and not going to start. Nobody is thanked. Nothing is forgiven. Nothing is resolved. The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and the nine miles is walked zero times. **The debts are the same four and are not a fifth, and the open items have moved from twenty-eight to thirty-three.**

# FROM `state/character-state.md`, 8 SECTION(S), 113805 BYTES, MOVED VERBATIM
## THE REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0001 — WHAT CHANGED IN THESE PEOPLE, AND IT IS FOUR DATES AND ONE ARGUMENT

*Appended by the run that took the review of Volume 07 Batch 0001 and applied it. Nothing above this line is rewritten, and every figure the repair changed is corrected in the rows above with the wrong one left standing beside it. **A repair is not a review and it discharges none of the four debts. No principal gained a motive, a decision, a relationship, an office, a name or a debt, and no thread was paid, and the four debts of every person in this section are exactly the four they were before the repair.** What follows is what a later writer must not inherit wrongly.*

**Marek Kest, thirty-eight. His one paid power event now carries the date Chapter 0299 pays it on.** The second day of the first week of the twelfth month of the year after the year after next, in a hired stone store four hundred yards off a bay — and three rows of this section, the page at Chapter 0305 and the outgoing prompt all said the second week of the twelfth month, and the page said the fifth year, which would have moved the event from before this volume opened to four weeks inside it. **All four now carry the chapter's own date.** Unchanged and not to be inherited otherwise: he holds no certification and has held none, he is in no office, no fund and no list, **a bar of his own came off him on the twelfth of the ninth month of last year on an application of one line which he wrote himself** and that sentence is paid in his own words and is not varied, **he is not at a stage and is not going to get to one, the reason he gives out loud is in about nine words, this volume exercises nothing, and a correct non-use is entered as one.** The mark on the inside of his left wrist is a symptom and not a power: it reads nothing, it decides nothing, it does not know where the ford is, and **the wage is the price and not the mark.** The three rows above that read *In the second week of the twelfth month he went to a hired stone store* have been corrected in place and the wrong reading stands once in each so that a later reader can see what moved.

**The man of about thirty-four with a fair hand. His two chapters are eight weeks apart, not ten, and no other part of him moved.** Chapter 0301 is the first of that batch's ten and Chapter 0309 is the ninth, and the step from each chapter to the next is exactly seven days. **The card, the page, the chapter's title, the chapter summary and the heading above all said ten and all now say eight.** He still does not ask anybody anything, he is still findable by a room, by a woman of about forty with a scuttle, by a woman of about thirty-five next door and by a stair four miles off, **the bill for his being findable is not his and stands on somebody else's account, he worked that out at second hand and did not go and look, and Chapter 0301 now makes that claim twice instead of three times**, once at second hand and once in the emphasis the card reserved for it, with the third restatement folded into the sentence that lists the three ways of being found. He still has not been thanked for anything and there is still no form in this empire that would let a person be thanked for saying a thing out loud in a room. His wife is still at the bottom of four steps and still does not ask him how the week went and still is not going to.

**Marn Ottery, thirty-four, a clerk at the fourth counter, eleven years, not a lawyer.** **Nothing in her changed and her chapter lost fifty-eight words it should never have had.** The opening of Chapter 0303 was lifted from closed Chapter 0292 and now describes the same counter, the same four flights, the same board of four things and not one of them a time, and the same about nine people a day, **from behind the counter instead of in front of it, and she is named in the second paragraph as her card asked and was not.** She still says the shape of a thing before she says the thing, about nine hundred times in eleven years and to a person twice. She still says the volume's first rule out loud in a room to nobody in particular. She still does not ask the man of forty-five one thing. She still puts a coin back on a counter once in eleven years rather than take it. **The box of about four hundred bought and unfilled blanks is still under that counter, still has no form anywhere that lets her keep it, is still the nearest thing to a post anybody has built in this empire, and is still not a post, and the only thing that would turn it into one is a person asking for it, and a person asking for it is told no by a person.** The fifth emphasis run her card named and the page had never placed is now placed, and it is her own sentence: a blank is the only piece of paper in this empire that cannot be held against anybody. Owed and unpaid: the answer to a question about a line of writing on a fee board that nobody can answer; the fourth thing on that board that she and two other clerks cannot account for; and about four hundred blanks' worth of nothing.

**Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, a clerk at the fourth counter for two years, and two boxes on a page ruled by hand in her own book with the same name in both.** **Her chapter had been arguing with itself and the repair settled it on the side her card and her finding were already on.** She had argued that a line in a book of her own about a person who cannot be got at is a window and then, ninety words later in overlapping wording, that the same line is a wall and not a window until somebody stands at it. **The finding is the window, the second verdict is gone, and what the emphasis now carries is the window and its cost, which is that nobody has ever stood at the other side of it.** She is still asked nothing, still was asked one question twelve weeks ago by a man who came up that stair wanting nothing and did not answer it, and still wants to be asked every working day. **A person who is not asked has not been given a duty, and she has known that since about the seventh month of next year**, and the repair removed a second copy of that date from the sentence in front of it and left the canon phrase whole. **Her undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, and its count is the sixth and not the fifth, because closed Chapter 0292 had already spent the fifth twelve weeks earlier, and the page had said the fifth time, the same five reasons, the first four and then the sixth reason, which was three numbers and one count.** The fourth thing in her drawer is untouched, unsent and unopened, with three others. **The decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is unmade, is not on the page, and this repair did not make it.** No romance begins and none is implied.

**Halla Wray, fifty-one, foreman of the Slade Cut.** **Unchanged, and one of her threads now has a room attached to it that was not settled.** She is in a box since the first week of the seventh month of the year after, entered by a class, and has never been asked about it. She has worked out that being in a box and not being asked about it are the same arrangement, and that the arrangement was made in a shed by nine families and not by her, and that the entries that put her there were about a class, and a class is a heading, and a heading is how a person becomes a company. She still asks nothing and is thanked by nobody. **The two of seventeen at the back of the bay are still unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for and not going to start, and the repair touched one emphasis run in her chapter and nothing else in it.** Owed and unpaid: the second sentence she owes a book-keeper four hundred yards up that lane, and the finding that a person is the one thing a class cannot be asked about. **New on the record: closed Chapter 0299 puts the man of about fifty-five with a chain in the hired stone store and Chapter 0306 puts him in the bay, and the repair made the chalk mark agree with the bay and did not move the man.**

**The man of about sixty-one, who signs at the foot of every sheet.** **Unchanged, and his ordinary day lost a figure that was never his.** He is sixty-one, the second hand since he was forty-two, cannot read a paragraph, signs about nine hundred sheets a year, has about nine of them in about nineteen years in his head that were not as the person above him wrote them, and wants about four minutes to say no to about half and yes to the rest, and there is nobody to ask. **Chapter 0307 had said about four hundred sheets a year going into about four hundred other rooms and one sheet in five going nowhere near the room; both are gone, the canon is about nine hundred sheets a year, and the line now says that not one of them comes back into it.** He said the whole of it out loud at a rail in a room in the second month of the fourth year and it went into a room and not into a book, and he is not a party and that is his whole protection and his whole loss, and **he is not a document, a list, a route or a symbol and this repair made him none of the four.**

**The clerk of about fifty-five in the office of a seat.** **Unchanged, and the one thing taken out of her chapter is a period she was never entitled to state.** She wrote a question into the second of the eleven books on her own motion, at nobody's request, with the day and the hour on it and her own name at the foot of it, on the fourth day of the third week of the ninth month of the year after next, **and a quarter of it was answered out loud in her room in the second month of the fourth year and she wrote none of that down either.** **Chapter 0310 used to say the question had been on that shelf about a year and now says it is still on that shelf under the window with the pane out of it and states no period at all, because `outline/volume-07.md` and the two closed chapters that carry the date are a year apart and a period would pick a side.** The season is on and nobody tells him. She has not entered an order in nineteen years and a minute with an answer in it is an order. Nobody is going to ask her a second time. Owed: the answer; the fact that she is the only person in this matter answerable in writing for a question and is paid nothing; and about four thousand entries in eleven books with no name in any one of them.

**The others, and what the repair did to them, which is nothing at all.** **Nell Kest, twenty, sixteen books, three women untold and a wage held since the second week of the third month of the year after: unchanged.** Her chapter lost a bolded absolute that its own seventeenth line refuted — a wage-day notice is no longer called the only piece of paper in this empire that says a woman is owed money, and what stands is the part that is true and is the point of the chapter, that nothing in this empire can hold a notice against anybody at all. **She is still not going up this river this season and has not said she is never going to, and she is still the only person who knows there is a difference between the two, and the collision with the woman about nine miles off the building off the old river road is still on the record and is still unresolved.** The woman of about twenty-six at the end of the cold passage: unchanged, except that a period of about nineteen weeks that contradicted her own date is gone and she will still not give the figure, will still not give it a heading, will still not ask her three questions, and is not coming up a stair. **The four men in a bay who cannot read a paragraph were not asked one question about a number and were not asked one after the repair either. The man of about fifty-five with a chain is still not one of them. The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen are still unspoken to, unthanked and unsent for. The two people in their boxes are still in them. The question in the second of the eleven books is still a question. The guarantee is still offered and unanswered with about sixty children inside it and no child in it named. The Lowcross bill still stands at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, and he did not pay it.**

---

## VOLUME 07, BATCH 0002 — WHAT MOVED IN THESE PEOPLE, AND WHAT DID NOT

*Appended by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0002/PROMPT.md`, after its last edit to any chapter, and nothing above this line is rewritten. **A self-audit and not a review, and it discharges none of the four debts. No principal gained a post, a certification, an office, a name, a motive that was not already on the page, or a debt that was not already owed. The four debts of every person below are exactly the four they were before this batch**, and what follows is what a later writer must not inherit wrongly.*

**Marn Ottery, thirty-four, a clerk at the fourth counter, eleven years, not a lawyer — and the movement is the one she is in the middle of, and her two chapters say the whole of it out loud.** **Chapter 0311** puts the only form in this empire ever put in front of about nine hundred rooms at once into a room at the top of a stair, out of a copy a man of about thirty-four carried up four flights for and paid fourpence for at the far end: a House's border law, about two hundred years old, requiring that a thing offered to a border household at no charge be answered, that the answer be entered, and that the entering of it be a form and not a letter. **It got an answer in about three weeks.** She says out loud, in her own mouth, and this is new on the page, **that it is not one of the seven things anybody has asked out loud in this matter, and why: no person was in it, nobody put it to anybody, and a household is not a person, so there was nobody in any of those rooms for anything to be done to.** **Chapter 0318** says the movement's finding out loud at about the fourth hour, and the finding is that a column with a heading over it is a machine and a column with no heading over it is a person, and she puts the proof under her own counter, where it has been for eleven years: about four hundred bought and unfilled blanks with no number over them, no day, no name and no heading. **She is not going to give them a heading, because a heading is what a company is made of.** **The box is still not a post, it is still not resolved, the fourth thing on the board behind her is still in a hand she and two other clerks cannot account for, and nobody thanked her and nobody is going to and there is not one form anywhere in this empire that obliges anybody to.** She did not ask the man of thirty-four one thing, or the woman of about twenty-nine one thing, or the man of about forty one thing. **How she knows about the border form: she has kept four copies of it in a drawer with the rest of the paper for about a year and nobody has ever asked her what they are, and the copies come down that counter about four times a year.** Owed and unpaid: the answer to a question about a line of writing on a fee board that nobody can answer; the fourth thing on that board; about four hundred blanks' worth of nothing; and the price of eleven years of being the person at the counter who does not ask.

**Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, a clerk at the fourth counter for two years, and two boxes on a page ruled by hand in her own book with the same name in both — and this movement is where she said the reason out loud.** **Chapter 0313**: the border form works because the law does not need a person, and a border household is not a person either, and the law was written by people who had noticed that about two hundred years ago and had better sense about it than we have. Then the half that took her two years: **the form asks about a class, a class is a heading, a heading is a company, so the one form in this empire that ever worked at a scale is the one form that makes about nine hundred rooms into a company, and nobody wanted that, and it has not been copied since.** **Chapter 0319**: the other half of the movement's finding, that the about nine hundred entries a year are about nine hundred classes and the second hand at the foot of every one of them is a person, and a person is the one thing in this empire that a class cannot be asked about. And then, in a rented room two streets off a street that goes down to the river road, **she worked out in about four minutes why she is not going to give the two boxes a heading: a heading is what a company is made of, and the name in them is a woman of thirty-four who has been at the other end of the same counter for eleven years and has never been asked about anything, and nobody has ever asked her.** She is not going to rule a third line. The undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, **and no new ordinal number was given to it in this batch, because the sixth was spent in an earlier month and a reprint of the count would compute a period out of it; the reason has not changed, which is that nobody asked her and nobody asked her this month either and there is nothing in a month in which nobody asks you to make a new reason out of.** **The decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is unmade, is not in this room, is not going to be made this month, and nobody in this empire is going to be told when it is made if it ever is.** The fourth thing in the drawer is untouched, unsent and unopened, with three others. She was asked nothing in this batch. **Owed and unpaid:** the answer she did not give; a fourth box, which she will not say about; the price of being a party, said out loud ten times and never once thanked; and the name at the top of the line, which is the name of a person who cannot be got at.

**The man of about thirty-four with the fair hand, four miles from the room over the chandler's shop, and his two chapters here are the movement's arithmetic and its empty column.** **Chapter 0312** is the finding worked out in a room with the lamp not lit: **a person deciding can say no, and this one said no, and it took about eleven weeks and it cost about a hundred and forty years, and that is not a slow answer, it is the answer you get when the thing doing the answering is somebody. A class cannot be frightened and cannot be told what a thing is going to be used for and cannot write anything down afterwards.** He did not go north, he did not go and look, he did not ask his wife anything, and the two of seventeen at the back of a bay and the two people in their boxes and the Lowcross bill and the guarantee are all in that chapter and none of them moved. **Chapter 0316** is a day-book four miles off and a correct sum in a third hand under a line he ruled himself: the hand is not his and is not the hand of either of the two people in his practice, and nobody is going to tell him whose it is and he is not going to go and look. And a column headed *next review* that has never had anything in it in about nine years, from which he works out **that a heading over an empty column is the most complete machine there is, because it gets looked at every single day and it never once gets asked anything.** He had it the other way round until this week. **A woman of about thirty-five keeps the day-book, came about nine miles twice, was not let in and wrote none of it down, and he did not ask her one thing about the third hand or about the heading.** **He went back the four miles on his own two feet. The bill for his being findable is not his and stands on somebody else's account. He has asked nobody anything and nobody thanked him.**

**Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, book-keeper of the Slade Cut Company, who has kept the Company's books since the second month of next year — and Chapter 0315 is his chapter and it is the first time this movement's finding has been on a piece of paper in a room four hundred yards off a bay.** He built a column in the second month of next year and it has no names in it, because a name in a column is a thing a person can be served at, and a person who can be served at is a party. **The other half of it is that he has never put a heading over it either, and he only got to that half this week, and he had it the wrong way round for about four months: it is the heading and not the names that makes a column a machine.** He wrote the correction out in a fair hand on a piece of waste paper and put the waste paper at the bottom of a box, and he read it twice and did not tear it up and has not shown it to anybody and is not going to. Nineteen days of a crew's money is in a weigh-house ledger about half a mile off and is called *held*, and there is no form in this empire that will let nineteen days of a wage be entered as owed by anybody, and he has looked and he has looked twice and he has looked with two other people. His own wages are held against a roof he put there on purpose and that one he can account for. **At the bottom of the lane: about four men in the bay cannot read a paragraph and were not asked one question about a number by him, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain was in there in the morning and said nothing and is not one of them and says so; the two of seventeen are at the back of that bay and he went up the lane past the door without going in and without stopping; the chalk mark on the wall is on a man's chain and means nothing and is about four years off being anybody's evidence and nothing converted it. The foreman of fifty-one was in the bay and he did not cross the lane to where she was standing, and the second sentence she owes him is still owed and he has not asked and is not going to.** Owed and unpaid: the second sentence; about nineteen days' money; the fourth thing on a fee board about four hundred yards away; and a mark in chalk that is about four years from being anybody's evidence.

**The man of about sixty-one who signs at the foot of every sheet — and Chapter 0320 is his chapter and the movement's finding reaches him as an ordinary morning.** He is sixty-one, the second hand since he was forty-two, signs about nine hundred sheets a year, and cannot read a paragraph. **He read the day on all of them this morning and the heading over the column on none of them, and the heading over the column is the name of a class, and a class is not a person, and a class cannot be frightened, cannot be told what a thing is going to be used for, and cannot write anything down afterwards, and that is the whole of why about nine hundred rooms a year can receive a sheet without anybody in any of them being made a party.** He is at the foot of it and he is a person. About nine of about nine hundred a year have not been what the person above them wrote and he signed those, and there is nobody to ask. He would want about four minutes. A carrier came up with a rack and said *same as yesterday*, which is the whole of the conversation there has been between them in about six years, and he is paid ninepence at a lock and neither of them has asked the other anything. **He said all of it out loud once, standing at a rail, and it went into a room and not into a book, and there is not one form anywhere in this empire that will let anybody ask the woman of about forty who was in that room what she heard. He is not one of the four men in a bay and he is not in any list with them and he does not know that they exist, and he is not a document, a list, a route or a symbol. The season is still on and he is about nine miles off and nobody is going to tell him and nobody walks the nine miles in this batch.** Owed and unpaid: the four minutes; the nine of them; and the absence of a column on any sheet for a man to write no in.

**The clerk of about fifty-five in the office of a seat — and Chapter 0314 is her chapter and the finding arrives at her as a comparison between her own books and a form she has been receiving for nineteen years.** Nineteen years in one room, writes the minutes, advises nobody, asks nothing, about four thousand entries in eleven books and not one name in any one of them. **About four of the border forms come down her counter a year, with a heading over a column and a space for the answer and the entering of it a form and not a letter, and the answers came back in about three weeks and they were refusals and the refusals were entered.** She says out loud, to a room with nobody else in it, that her eleven books are the cheapest way anybody in this empire has found of putting a question in front of a building, and that not one of about four thousand entries has ever asked anybody anything at all, and that **the whole of the difference between her books and that form is one word and the word is a name, and that no form in this empire turns an entry with no name in it into a person anybody could answer to, and that is not a gap in the system, that is the system.** **A book is a room.** The question in the second of the eleven books is still a question and carries no period for how long it has been on the shelf, because the outline and the two closed chapters that carry its date are a year apart, and she wrote none of the quarter of it that was answered out loud in her room in the second month down either, and nobody is going to ask her a second time. **The season is on and he is about nine miles off and there is no form anywhere in this empire that would let anybody in that room tell him so, and nobody is going to be the one to try.** Owed and unpaid: the answer; the fact that she is the only person in this matter answerable in writing for a question and is paid nothing; and about four thousand entries with no name in any of them.

**The woman of about twenty-six at the end of the cold passage — and Chapter 0317 is her chapter and what she found this week is the sixth ruled space, and she gave it nothing.** She counts the racks that come back light by one, in her head, without touching the rack, and the figure has gone up in every year since the seventh month of the year after and she has not said by how much and is not going to, and the figure is a count of carriers and not of people and she is not going to correct it. **A sheet came down the passage with a rule on it she has seen on about four other things, and it belongs to a book kept at a clerk's window about four hundred and thirty miles down this river, and at the bottom of every page of that book, under five columns, there is a sixth ruled space that has never had a heading over it and never had anything in it, and about two hundred and forty people who have lived in that district all their lives have never known it is there.** She worked out in about four minutes that the five columns are machines and the sixth is not, and then that the sixth is not a person either, because nothing has ever been put in it and nobody has ever been introduced to it, **and that it is the closest thing this empire has to a person that nobody has ever been introduced to, and that the whole of what she wanted out of a page in four years is sitting at the bottom of somebody else's.** She is not giving her own figure a heading. Three questions for the man of about sixty-one, none asked. Not coming up a stair, and not walking the four hundred yards, and the figure is not printed. Owed and unpaid: the figure she will not give and will not hand on; three questions she will not ask; and the third word on the slate, which exists and has no name on it.

**And the others, every debt this batch leaves standing on them, and nothing here is resolved.** **The four men in a bay who cannot read a paragraph, unnamed, still never asked one question about a number in any of the six closed volumes and in none of these ten chapters, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain among them is not one of them and says so and was not put with them.** The reader of seventeen at the back of that bay, unnamed, unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, not going to start. The girl of seventeen in the same bay, in the same condition, who would say yes and has never been given the chance to say no, and a chapter that thanks her breaks this volume and no chapter of this batch did. A man of about thirty from the second gang, a party. A man of about thirty with fourpence in his hand who did not spend it. A man of about forty-four who gave the man of thirty-four a copy he had had about eleven years and did not know what it was for. A woman of about thirty-two who asks about a letter and a day and is told the truth and is not told a kindness. A woman of about twenty-nine whose day has passed. A man of about forty who wanted three things put back a year. A man of about fifty who wanted a thing put back two years. A man of about thirty-four who came up four flights in Chapter 0311 and put his hand flat on a copy on a counter and took it off again. A man of about thirty-four who came up four flights in Chapter 0318 and paid fourpence for a page and told nobody what the page was of. A woman of about thirty-five with a day-book in a lodging house four miles off, who came about nine miles twice, was not let in, wrote none of it down, and was not asked one thing. A woman of about forty with a scuttle who lit a lamp early once and is not in this matter and cannot be asked what she heard. A woman of about thirty who has money and offered some of it and was refused. A boy of about nine with two slates, the second one with an office question on it and no name on it, and nobody has thanked him and nobody ever has. A foreman off a dye end holding a notice in a coat. About nine men who know a foreman of fifty-one is in a box and have not said a word about it. A House of about two hundred years ago that wrote a border law and is not named. **The man of about thirty-four's wife, unnamed, at the bottom of four steps, not thanked, has not been thanked, did not ask him how the week went, and said out loud some weeks ago that she is not going to. The two documents stay two documents four hundred and thirty miles apart and neither is named in any of these ten files. And two boxes that stay shut, a guarantee that stands unanswered with about sixty children inside it and no child named in it, and a bill at nineteen pounds three and fourpence that nobody is liable for.**

**A closing note on the two chapters this batch wrote twice, and a later writer should not read the repetition as a lapse.** Marn Ottery has two and Tamsin Rook has two, and both of Tamsin's are eight weeks apart at the same counter, and the point of both is that the same woman at the same counter has now said the whole of a thing out loud twice. **The three paragraphs that were exactly duplicated between the two of them were reworded because a duplicated-paragraph test and a forty-word window both returned them, and the second chapter now describes the room from a different side of it.** What did not change between them: the room, the board of four things and not one of them a time, the man at the far end doing copies at fourpence, the woman of about twenty at the back, and the four things in the drawer, none of which was opened.

---

## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 — WHAT CHANGED IN THESE PEOPLE, AND IT IS ONE PHRASE IN A DATE, ONE ROOM, AND NOBODY

*Appended by the run that read `logs/batch-0002.review.log` and the ten files, after its last edit to any chapter. Nothing above this point is rewritten. **This section changed no person's history, no one's debt and no one's standing. It changed the words six chapters use to describe the same people, and one chapter's date, and that is the whole of it.***

- **Marn Ottery, thirty-four, a clerk at the fourth counter, eleven years, not a lawyer.** **Chapter 0311 is her chapter and this repair rewrote its middle.** The man of about thirty-four who came up four flights with a copy now asks her why she is telling him, and she answers with three reasons that are about him and one that is a cost to her, and the answer is not a fourth statement of the movement's finding. **The woman of about twenty-nine whose day has passed now has a book on the shelf behind that counter that would have the answer in it, and nobody asked her to open it and nothing in this empire would make anybody ask her to, and Marn Ottery did not open it.** The man of about forty who wanted three things put back a year has the three of them on one sheet. **What is owed her is exactly what it was: the four hundred blanks under her counter, the fourth thing on the board in a hand that is not hers, the four hundred and thirty miles down the river, and the fact that she has said the whole of this out loud to a person twice in eleven years and been thanked for it a number of times that is nil. She is not thanked by this repair either and the count of askings is seven.**
- **The man of about thirty-four with a fair hand, and the bill, and the eighth question.** **He is on the page in Chapter 0311 as the person who asks her why she is telling, and the question is printed without a question mark because a question mark in this matter is not the count and the count is a question put out loud in a room, and this one is in the room and is not the count.** He still has the bill that is not his, still has the four days a season, still is owed a form in this empire for asking a person whether they will mind being told something, and still is owed the thing nobody thanked him for. **The copy went down the four flights with him, fourpence paid, a sheet of paper and a thing said out loud in a room, and that is what passed between them, and the chapter is 192 words longer than it was and the 192 words are the question, its answer, the book on the shelf and the three things on one sheet.**
- **Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, at the other end of the same counter, about two years, not a lawyer.** **Chapter 0319 was lifting four paragraphs out of Chapter 0313 and this repair reworded all four, and one of them was the two things under her table and one of them was the line in her own hand recording that a clerk of thirty-four said no and gave no reason, and the fact is in both chapters and the wording is now in each of them only.** The two boxes drawn by hand with the same name in both, the third page she is not going to rule a line on, the four things in the drawer, the lamp she lit early, and the four minutes she needed for something she had known for two years are all exactly where Batch 0002 left them. **She is still not going to give those boxes a heading, and a heading is still what a company is made of, and the woman of thirty-four at the other end of the counter is still in them.**
- **The clerk of about fifty-five, nineteen years in one room, four thousand entries and not one name.** **Chapter 0314 was carrying about a hundred and twenty contiguous words out of Chapter 0310 and this repair re-cut all of it, and the facts are the same facts: the room off the old river road, the shelf, the eleven books, the table, the chair, the two-foot window, the pane out of it for about four years and nobody to ask about the pane, the passage, the cold flags, the rail, the step worn through, the four thousand entries with no name in any one of them, the question in the second of the eleven books, and the carrier at ninepence a lock who has not been asked what she is carrying in about six years.** **One phrase of hers did not survive and it is not hers: the question she wrote is now dated, in this repair's corrected outline and in this batch's page alike, to the ninth month of the year after next, where two closed chapters already dated it, and it is the same question and the same clerk and the same nineteen years.**
- **The man of about sixty-one, the second hand, about nine hundred sheets a year, and the season.** **Untouched.** Chapter 0320 lost one lifted sentence about a door and a step and kept every word about him, and he is still about nine miles off, still not going to be told, still signed four sheets before the light came properly, and the season is still on.
- **The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen, about four hundred and thirty miles down this river.** **Untouched, and still unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, and not going to start**, and the two of them are still at the back of a bay on a written engagement at the rate a list is set at.
- **The ones this repair did not touch at all**, and they are the long row: the four men in the bay who cannot read a paragraph, the foreman of fifty-one, the foreman off a dye end about four hundred and thirty miles up the river, the woman of about twenty-six at the end of the cold passage, the woman of about forty with a scuttle, the woman of about thirty-five next door, the reader of seventeen, the man of about fifty-five with a chain, and the two hundred and forty people in that district who have never known the sixth ruled space is there.


---

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0003, CHAPTERS 321 TO 330 — WHAT CHANGED IN THESE PEOPLE, AND IT IS ONE FINDING, ONE VISIT, ONE PAID WALK AND FIVE ROOMS

*Appended by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, after its last edit to any chapter file. Nothing above this point is rewritten. This section changed no person's history, no one's debt and no one's standing. It moved one man nine miles on his own two feet and put one question in one mouth and did not take it out, and that is the whole of it.*

- **The man of about thirty-four with a fair hand, and the thing he has been carrying.** **His finding turned over in Chapter 0321 and that is the only thing that changed about him in this batch.** He had carried it for about two years as a document — a way to ask one man about about nine hundred sheets a year — and it is not a document: a document goes to about nine hundred rooms and a question has to be said in one of them, and **a room is the only kind of thing in this empire a person can be said in.** He had it eight weeks ago as a hand at the foot of a sheet and stopped there, because the next step is a step out of a room and not into one. **Chapter 0323 is the first time he has been in the room itself, and he walked there on his own two feet on a day he is not paid for, and nobody sent him, and he is not on a list and does not work in that building, and nobody asked him why he was there and no form in this empire would let anybody ask.** He had the question ready since the fourth week of the second month and it was in his mouth at about the sixth minute of the nine minutes and it stayed there. **He gave the reason out loud in five sentences, to the room, and it was not a speech, and the reason is the movement's finding and not his opinion: a question put in that room goes into a record and the record is what the sheet goes in, and the last time a thing of that shape was done to about four hundred people their sheet went back, and a question put anywhere else costs nine miles and makes the man findable by the going, and a man who is findable is a man who can be served.** He did not ask it, and nobody thanked him for not asking it, and he did not tell anybody afterwards what was said in that room. **His walk is not given a distance on the page, deliberately, so that the outline's ninth-mile third visit in the fifth movement is not contradicted by a figure this movement has already spent; the distance is available to Batch 0004 and Batch 0005 and is not yet on the page.** Still owed and still unpaid: the bill at Lowcross at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, which is not his and is named once in this batch and is in Chapter 0321's list; the form in this empire for asking a person whether they will mind being told something, which does not exist and which is named again in Chapter 0330; the thing nobody thanked him for; and the eighth question, which is his to ask and which he has not asked.
- **The man of about sixty-one, the second hand, about nine hundred sheets a year, and the third time.** **He came, in Chapter 0323, and that is the whole of what changed and it is not a small thing.** About nine miles, in a season, on his own two feet, on a day he is not paid for, and nobody sent him and nobody told him, and there is no form in this empire that would let anybody in that building say that a person was expected. He stood on the wrong side of the rail for about four minutes and then put a sheet down on the rail itself, which is a thing he has done twice now and which no form in this empire records a person doing, and said about nine minutes of it standing up. **He has been in that room three times in nineteen years and the whole of it has now been said out loud twice in nineteen years by one man, at two of those three visits and not on the first, and the two figures are two figures: closed Chapter 0260 is the second visit and in it he says out loud that he has never said it out loud in a room, which is what fixes the count, and the repair of this batch made the page say so in his own mouth. And a man who has said a thing twice is findable by the saying of it, and about nine people were in that room, and the clerk of about fifty-five wrote none of it down and said out loud that writing it down would make it an order, and nobody thanked anybody and nobody has ever thanked him and there is not one form anywhere in this empire that requires a person to be thanked.** He picked the sheet up and gave it back and she entered it correctly in a hand that does not vary. He went out by the same door and down the same four hundred yards of cold flags on his own two feet and nobody walked with him. **He is still sixty-one, still the second hand since he was forty-two, still cannot read a paragraph, still has about nine of about nine hundred a year in his head that he cannot write down, still wants about four minutes, and still has nobody to ask, and the season is still on, and one of the sheets going out of that room before the end of it goes to the district he lives in and his hand will be at the foot of it and he will not be told.** He is not one of the four men in a bay and does not know that they exist and is not in any list with them. He is not the who.
- **The clerk of about fifty-five, nineteen years in one room, four thousand entries and not one name.** **Chapter 0322 is her chapter in this batch and it is the floor under the movement.** She said out loud, to a page, that she is not going to ask him anything, and gave the reason, and the reason is the finding rather than a discovery she made this year: **a question put in that building is a question put into a record, and the record is nineteen years of a woman of fifty-five copying a hand into eleven books with no name in any of them, and the sheet goes in there, and a person who is asked in that building is a person who has been served at, and the last time a thing of that shape was done to about four hundred people their sheet went back.** A minute with an answer in it is an order and she has not entered one in nineteen years. **And the two are one fact and not two: the arrangement that has kept about nine hundred sheets a year correct for nineteen years is the arrangement that has kept anybody from asking him one question, and if they were two there would be something to be done about the second one.** In Chapter 0323 she wrote none of what he said down and said out loud that writing it down would make it an order. In Chapter 0324 she said the cost out loud and put the pen down and did not pick it up again that afternoon, which she has not done in nineteen years. The question in the second of the eleven books is still a question, is still on the shelf under the window with the pane out of it, and nobody is going to ask her a second time. **She is still the only person in this matter who is answerable in writing for a question and is paid nothing for it**, and the pane is still out, and the step is still worn through, and the rail is still about four feet high. Owed and unpaid: the answer; about four thousand entries in eleven books with no name in any one of them; and a man of about thirty-four was in her room and she is not allowed to ask him what he was doing there.
- **Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, at the other end of the same counter, about two years, not a lawyer.** **Chapter 0328 is her chapter and what it gives her is a cost and not an event.** Two instruments require her in any room he is in and they are not one instrument and neither has been merged with the two boxes she has drawn by hand. **If he had gone up a stair to the building off the old river road she would have been in that room, and that is what the two of them are for and it is certain, and he is about nine miles off and did not go, and she is not told, and there is no form in this empire that would let anybody tell her that he did not go.** The undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, and no new ordinal was given to it in this batch, and the reason is the same one it has been, which is that nobody asked her. **The inherited sentence is used in its own wording this once — a person who is not asked has not been given a duty, and she has known that since about the seventh month of next year — and no elapsed period was computed from it.** The four things in the drawer are unopened and the fourth of them is the one she has never looked at. The two boxes are unheaded and she is not going to head them. The third page is unruled. **There is no form in this empire in which a woman can be told that a thing did not happen**, and the not being told is the ordinary condition of a person who is not a party and is also the whole of what the two instruments have cost her, which is nothing. **The decision about whether she trusts a man's judgment is unmade, is not on the page, this batch did not make it and may not make it for her, and no romance begins anywhere in this batch and none is implied.** Owed and unpaid: the answer she did not give; a fourth box, if there is one, which she will not say; and the price of being a party, said out loud ten times and never once thanked.
- **Halla Wray, fifty-one, foreman of the Slade Cut, nine years of a thing nine families agreed to in the open.** **Chapter 0326 is her chapter and the movement reached her as a fact about a room she is not in.** A man of about thirty told her one thing at second hand in about nine words: a question cannot be put in the one building that makes the man who signs at the foot of a sheet. **She said one sentence out loud about it, in a bay, to about nine men, and it is the same sentence she said about herself a long time ago, and this is the second time, and it is not a coincidence and it is not a system** — she has been in a box since the first week of the seventh month of the year after, entered by a class, and nobody has ever asked her about it, and the reason is that nobody can be asked about a box. **She said it once and the chapter did not compare the two cases and did not make her an instrument by saying it, and nine mouths that have said nothing for about four years said nothing again.** Still in the box, still not asked, still owed a second sentence to a book-keeper four hundred yards up that lane and still not asking for it, and the day he asks is the day she stops saying the half she has got. The mark in chalk on that wall is a mark and means nothing and is nobody's and is about four years off being anybody's evidence and is not converted. **The girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen are at the back of her bay and neither was spoken to, thanked or sent for in this batch, and neither is going to start, and the chapter that carries them does not narrate their silence as a cost they are bearing.**
- **Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, book-keeper of the Slade Cut Company.** **Chapter 0327 is his chapter and what it gives him is the absence of an instrument.** He still has the finding on a piece of waste paper at the bottom of a box, in a fair hand, about four months late, and nobody has seen it and he is not going to show it to anybody, because no form in this empire lets a man who has written the right of a matter down on waste paper have it received by anybody. **What the movement gave him instead is the knowledge that the one man in this matter who could answer any of it is about nine miles off a room he has no way into and has no business in, and that there is no form in this empire for asking a man in a room to wait** — and asking a man to wait is asking a man to stay findable, and a man who waits is a man who can be served. Nineteen days of a crew's money is still in a weigh-house ledger about half a mile off called *held*, three pounds and eleven and threepence, and his own wages are still held against a roof he put there on purpose, **and the two are not the same thing and he has never once put them in one column.** The fourth thing on a fee board about four hundred yards away is still in a hand he cannot account for and there is still no form in this empire for accounting for a line of writing on a wall. A column in the second of the Company's papers is still ruled and empty and unheaded. **Nobody asked him anything in this batch and nobody thanked him, and the foreman of fifty-one still owes him a second sentence and he is still not going to ask.**
- **The woman of about twenty-six at the end of the cold passage.** **Chapter 0325 is her chapter and the one new thing she has is a fact that buys her nothing.** He came a third time, and she was in the building, and she is the only other person in this matter who knows what he said at that rail, and she has known it once and now knows he has said the whole of it twice. **The fact does not give her a fourth question and it does not get her out of a corridor, and she is not coming up a stair in this volume, and nobody has written down that she is going to.** She has three questions for the man of about sixty-one and none of them has been asked and none is going to be. The figure she has counted in her head for about four years has gone up in every year since the seventh month of the year after, has not been said by how much, is a count of carriers and not of people, is not going to be corrected, has not been given a heading and is not going to be. Owed: the figure she will not give; the three questions she will not ask; and the passage, which she is not coming up out of.
- **Nell Kest, twenty, dye-house worker, sixteen books, a furnace town about four hundred and thirty miles up this river.** **Chapter 0329 is her chapter and what it gives her is a finding about eleven houses and nobody to tell it to.** The only question in this matter ever put in front of about nine hundred rooms at once was put there by a House's border law, and a border household is not a person and the law does not need a person, **and her eleven houses off a dyers' lane are not a person either, and so there is nothing in this empire that can be put in front of eleven houses at once and get an answer.** She worked that out in about four minutes at about the fourth hour with a pen in her hand and then had to sit there with the rest of it for a while. **The foreman in the coat with the notice in it is a different man and was not told any of it and is not going to be, and the notice is still in the coat.** Her wage is still held since the second week of the third month of the year after, three of the four women are still untold, the money from the woman of about thirty is still refused, and she is still not going up this river this season and has still not said she is never going to, and she is still the only person who knows there is a difference between the two. **The other held wage in this matter, about nine miles off a building off the old river road, belongs to a different woman who has told a different woman something else, and the two are kept apart on purpose, and the woman about nine miles off does not appear in Chapter 0329.**
- **The ones this batch did not touch, and they are the long row and every one of them is exactly where it was.** The four men in a bay who cannot read a paragraph, unnamed, none of them asked one question about a number. **The man of about fifty-five with a chain, who is in that bay in the morning and says out loud, to nobody in particular, where the number four comes from, and who says he is not one of the four and that putting him down as one of them would be a way for a list of four to go wrong, and that he has known for about four years that findability does not work on him and has never once used it and wants it said that he knew what he was doing.** A reader of seventeen at the back of that bay on a written engagement. **A girl of seventeen at the back of the same bay, unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, not going to start, and she would say yes and has never been given the chance to say no.** A man of about thirty from the second gang, findable. A man of about thirty with fourpence in his hand who did not spend it and has a wife. A man of about forty-four. A man of about thirty-four who came four miles in the rain and could not finish a question and did not come back. A woman of about thirty-five with a day-book in a lodging house who came about nine miles twice, was not let in, and wrote none of it down. **A woman of about forty with a scuttle, who was in the room at the rail for the whole of the nine minutes and said nothing and is not one of the three clerks and cannot be asked what she heard and is not going to be.** A woman of about thirty who has money and offered some of it. A boy of about nine with two slates, the second one with the office question on it and no name on it, and nobody has thanked him and nobody ever has. A woman of about twenty-nine whose day has passed and whose book on the shelf is not opened. Marn Ottery at the fourth counter, eleven years, not in this batch's ten chapters and not sent for and not spoken to. Marek Kest, thirty-eight, not in this batch's ten chapters, at no stair, nobody sent him, and nothing in this batch exercises anything.

---

# VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — THE PEOPLE AS THEY STAND AFTER CHAPTERS 0331 TO 0340

**Written by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. Nothing above this point is rewritten. Every one of these people is a person and not a device, and none of them is thanked in this batch and none of them is sent for, and the count of askings did not move.**

- **The man of about thirty-four with a fair hand, thirty-four or thereabouts, eleven years of copying out other men's figures so that the totals agree, four days a season on somebody's list, a room over a chandler's shop taken by the week, four steps and a landing, a house with a door on it four miles off.** **Chapter 0331 is his chapter of this batch and the hinge of it: he comes up a lane off the Slade, stops at about a hundred yards of a bay, can see into it through a door the weather has opened, and does not go the hundred yards.** That is the first time he has been within a hundred yards of either of the two people of seventeen at the back of it and he does not go in and nobody asks him why and he does not say. He gives the reason out loud in an empty lane in three turns, and the reason is the fourth rule and not a speech: **a person who cannot be read cannot be asked in writing, a person who cannot be asked cannot be told anything, and the arrangement that keeps about four men in a bay out of every book in this empire is the arrangement that loses about nine sheets a year nobody can account for, and it is not a right and not a method and nobody is going to be using it.** He has been within a hundred yards of a bay once in nineteen years of carrying this and has been owed the finding for two years. **In Chapter 0339 he is not at a stair, nobody sent him, nothing in this empire would let anybody send him, he does not go and look, he does not go north, the four hundred and thirty miles is not walked and the nine miles is not walked by anybody in this chapter, and he does not light the lamp.** In Chapter 0340 he puts the volume's promise in the negative in his own room: a question cannot be asked in about nine hundred buildings at once by people who cannot be instructed to ask it, and the reason is that a question in this matter is a party and a party is a person and a person is one. **Owed and unpaid: the bill at Lowcross, which is named in none of this batch's ten files; a form in this empire for asking a person whether they will mind being told something, which does not exist; the thing nobody thanked him for; and a wife at the foot of four steps who is not thanked either.** He walked nine miles once in this matter, in Chapter 0323, and not at all in this batch.

- **Halla Wray, fifty-one, foreman of the Slade Cut, nine years of a thing nine families agreed to in the open.** **Chapters 0332 and 0338 are her chapters of this batch and they are a day and a day.** Chapter 0332 is the day nobody asked, written flat and not as a near miss: nobody in that bay put a question to her about the box she has been in since the first week of the seventh month of the year after, and the man with the fair hand four hundred yards up the lane did not come up it and did not ask her for the second half-sentence. **The not asking is not a decision anybody took in a room, and being asked is being served, and a thing that cannot be refused is not a question, and she has wanted the lane to stay at four hundred yards for about four years.** In Chapter 0338 she says nothing new; the man of about fifty-five with the chain says nothing, or says the same thing he has said for about four years.
