## THE THREADS THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 OPENS, AND THE ONE IT CLOSES, AT CHAPTER 320

*Appended by the run that read `logs/batch-0002.review.log` and the ten files, after its last edit to any chapter. Nothing above this point is rewritten. Every row is in the present tense and every row carries a payoff target or an explicit em dash, and this section changes no page and moves no count.*

1. **The date of the question in the second of the eleven books, and the prohibition it carried, and both are closed.** The outline dated it a year later than closed Chapters 0260 and 0295 date it, and the three records that carried the contradiction also carried a prohibition on stating any elapsed period from it. **The outline now reads the ninth month of the year after next, the two closed chapters are the attestation, and the prohibition is lifted on the evidence.** The figure on the page is about fifty-eight weeks, as of closed Chapter 0295. Payoff target: any Volume 07 chapter that needs an elapsed period from it, stated in the project's own idiom and never as a month-count.
2. **Chapter 0303's year name, and it is closed.** The chapter carried the sixth-year name where its own card, its own summary, the character state and the outline all carried the fifth, and it now carries the fifth. **The count of figures corrected with it is three, in three files, and they stand beside the wrong ones rather than replacing them.** Payoff target: em dash. Nothing in Volume 07 is waiting on it and nothing in a later volume is either.
3. **The verbatim re-use, inside this batch and across the boundary into the batch that closed, and it is closed.** About one hundred and twenty contiguous words ran from Chapter 0310 into Chapter 0314, four blocks ran from Chapter 0313 into Chapter 0319, and twelve whole paragraphs ran out of Chapters 0303, 0304, 0307 and 0308 into Chapters 0311, 0313, 0317, 0318 and 0320. The batch's own record printed both window sweeps as zero and its duplicate-paragraph test as clean. **All of it is reworded in the later file of every pair with every fact kept, the duplicated paragraphs across the twenty chapters of the volume are zero, and the sixty-word and forty-word windows return zero inside the batch and against the other three hundred and one chapters.** Payoff target: em dash, with one exception, which is the next row.
4. **A duplication test that reported clean on about six hundred words of re-use, and it is open, and it is a method and not a batch.** A duplicated-paragraph test cannot see a duplication that runs to the end of a paragraph in one file and stops short of the next in the other; a forty-word window pointed only at a batch's own files cannot see a run against the batch that closed; and a duplicate-paragraph test pointed at a batch's own files cannot see a paragraph that the batch lifted out of the batch before it. **All three blinds were open at once in the same batch, and the record printed a zero on two sweeps and a clean on the third.** The battery now carries all three written down, which is what a blind-spot section is for. Payoff target: every batch that closes, and the standing check list, which is not a writer's to maintain.
4b. **The aggregate density the `about nine` bound cannot see, and it is open.** The bound measures the worst single phrase and returns a pass, and it cannot see the density a reader feels: 34 / 26 / 27 / 27 / 19 / 21 / 29 / 25 / 26 / 28 instances of `about <word or number>` across these ten files, one every fifty-seven words in the first, on a closed chapter twenty-seven. **The measure is added to the standing checks and the next writer is handed it; the texture is the manuscript's and is not restyled by a repair.** Payoff target: the density figure printed per batch from Batch 0003 onward, and no change to the page is owed by anybody.
5. **The single-sentence paragraph share, and it is open, and it is the manuscript's.** Thirty of forty-two prose paragraphs in Chapter 0311 hold one sentence, and closed Chapter 0301 holds twenty-four of thirty-nine, so the practice is three hundred chapters old and predates every batch that inherited it. `AGENTS.md` asks for two to six with a one-liner reserved for a deliberate beat, and the two have been disagreeing for a volume and more. Payoff target: a decision by an owner of the manuscript rather than a repair on a batch, and no batch is to be restyled for it.
6. **Emphasis as texture, and it is half open.** The count of runs is the manuscript's and Chapter 0301 carries fourteen, but two figures in this batch's own record do not reproduce: ninety-six printed against ninety-nine measured, and a joint-one ceiling of 13.1 against 13.12 measured in a file no repair touched. **Both readings stand in the records and neither is deleted.** Payoff target: the unnamed joint in the emphasis measure, which is the older of the two debts and is carried in `state/continuity.md`.
7. **The review gate, and it is open, and it is the oldest item in this file.** The gate falls back to the writing agent on every phase, so no review in this project is independent, and `reviews/volume-07-batch-0002.md` says so in its first section instead of claiming a second reading it did not get. **Volume 07 Batch 0001 has a review claimed in its own batch summary and no review file, and that gap is now named in `reviews/README.md` rather than left to be assumed.** The fix is one line of controller frontmatter. Payoff target: a controller owner, and it is not a writer's decision and not a repair's.
8. **The state layer's bound, and it is closed for this batch and open for the next.** The six rolling files stood at 1,225,227 bytes against about nine hundred and forty thousand and are 773,840, and five blocks of Volume 06's record are in `state/archive/` verbatim, with pointers in all five files and a new section in the archive README. **The rule holds: a rolling file carries the open volume, the two previous batches and the standing locks, and a closed volume is read in the archive.** Payoff target: Batch 0003's own sections, at about a hundred thousand bytes, which the headroom covers once and will not cover twice, and which would not have been covered at all three batches ago.
9. **The count of askings, and it is seven, and this repair did not move it.** Chapter 0311 gained a question that is a person putting a question to another person, printed without a question mark in the house form this matter uses for an asking that is not an asking, and the count stands. Payoff target: Chapter 0346 to 0348, where the man of thirty-four has the eighth question ready and does not ask it.
10. **The batch's thesis, and it is unchanged and it is not owed to a repair.** A column with a heading over it is a machine and a column with no heading over it is a person, and the about nine hundred entries a year are about nine hundred classes, and the second hand at the foot of every one of them is a person, and a person is the one thing in this empire that a class cannot be asked about. **This repair moved no sentence of it and contradicted none of it.** Payoff target: Chapters 0341 to 0350, where the answer is given in the negative in a room and not in a book.

## THE THREADS VOLUME 07 BATCH 0003 OPENS, AND THE ONE IT CLOSES, AT CHAPTER 330

*Appended by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, after its last edit to any chapter file. Nothing above this point is rewritten. Every row is in the present tense and every row carries a payoff target or an explicit em dash, and this section changes no page and moves no count.*

1. **The third rule, and it is the movement's finding and not a new thread.** *The address cannot be asked in the building that made it, and the arrangement that has kept about nine hundred sheets a year correct for nineteen years is the arrangement that has kept anybody from asking him one question, and those are one fact and not two.* Established in Chapter 0321, floored in Chapter 0322, staged in Chapter 0323, paid in Chapter 0324, and restated once in Chapter 0330 in different words. Payoff target: Chapters 0341 to 0350, in the negative, in a room.
2. **The cost, and it is a new fact and it is not money.** *A man who has said a thing twice is findable by the saying of it, and about nine people were in that room, and the clerk wrote none of it down, and nobody paid anything.* Chapter 0324. **The whole of it has now been said out loud twice in nineteen years, and no form in this empire records the act of a man standing at a rail, and nobody was thanked then and nobody has been thanked since.** Payoff target: the next volume's question.
3. **The third visit, and it is spent.** *He came for the third time in nineteen years, on his own two feet, in a season, on a day he is not paid for, and nobody sent him and nobody told him.* Chapter 0323. **The nine miles are walked once in this volume and the road is not walked again.** Payoff target: em dash.
4. **The eighth question, available and not taken.** *The man of about thirty-four had the question ready since the fourth week of the second month and it was in his mouth at about the sixth minute of the nine and it stayed there, and he gave the reason out loud in five sentences and did not ask it.* Chapter 0323. **The count of askings is seven before and seven after.** Payoff target: Chapters 0346 to 0348, where the outline puts it in his mouth for the last time.
5. **A man of about thirty-four in a room he has no business in, and the fact that nobody can ask him why.** Chapter 0323, and in Chapter 0324 the clerk of about fifty-five notes that he is not on a list and works nowhere in the building and that no form would let her ask. **He has now been in that room once and his walk is not given a distance on the page, deliberately, so that the outline's ninth-mile third visit in the fifth movement is not contradicted by a figure this movement has already spent.** Payoff target: Chapters 0331 to 0350.
6. **A woman of about forty with a scuttle, and about nine people's worth of nothing.** *She was in the room for the nine minutes and said nothing at any point in them and said nothing after, and she is not one of the three clerks, and there is not one form in this empire that will let anybody ask her what she heard, and nobody asked her and nobody is going to.* Chapters 0323 and 0324. Payoff target: —, and it is not going to be.
7. **A new fact in the hands of the woman of about twenty-six, and it buys nothing.** *He came a third time, and the fact does not give her a fourth question and does not get her out of a corridor, and she is not coming up a stair in this volume.* Chapter 0325. **Her figure has gone up in every year since the seventh month of the year after, has not been said by how much, is a count of carriers and not of people, is not going to be corrected, and has not been given a heading and is not going to be.** Payoff target: — and Movement 5 is not this one.
8. **The reversal on a bank four hundred and thirty miles down this river, said once.** *A question cannot be put in the one building that made him, and the same arrangement kept a foreman of fifty-one from being asked about a box for about four years, and she said that out loud once in a bay to about nine men and the chapter did not compare the two cases.* Chapter 0326. **The second sentence she owes a book-keeper four hundred yards up the lane is still owed and still unpaid, and the day he asks is the day she stops saying the half she has got.** Payoff target: Chapters 0331 to 0340.
9. **A piece of waste paper nobody has seen, and the absence of any instrument to show it on.** Chapter 0327, and it is still at the bottom of the box and still his own opinion and nobody has ever asked him for it. **Nineteen days of a crew's money is still in a weigh-house ledger about half a mile off called *held*, and his own wages are still held against a roof he put there on purpose, and the two have never been in one column and must not be.** The fourth thing on a fee board about four hundred yards away is still in a hand he cannot account for. Payoff target: —.
10. **The cost side of the third rule, and it is nothing, and it is hers.** *If he had gone up a stair she would have been in that room, and he did not go, and she is not told, and there is no form in this empire that would let anybody tell her that he did not go.* Chapter 0328. **The undertaking of the fourth of the first month is live, unretired, unamended and unexercised, no new ordinal was given to it, and the reason is still that nobody asked her.** Payoff target: —, and the sixth time is the last time the count moves and it did not move.
11. **Eleven houses that are not a person either.** Chapter 0329, and it is a finding she has nobody to tell and a foreman in a coat who is not told. **The other held wage in this matter, about nine miles off a building off the old river road, belongs to a different woman who has told a different woman something else, and the two of them are kept apart on purpose and the collision in this layer is still on the record and is still unresolved.** Payoff target: —.
12. **The price handed to the fourth movement, and the fourth rule is not yet in a mouth.** *The only people in this matter who cannot be reached are the people nobody has asked, and the price of that is that they are also the people who are never told anything.* Chapter 0330 hands it on as a price and does not state the fourth rule in a mouth and does not state it in a book. Payoff target: Chapters 0331 to 0340, in a mouth, and a chapter that makes the four a route breaks this volume as surely as a chapter that thanks the girl of seventeen does.
13. **The season, and the one sheet, and the district.** *About nine hundred sheets go out of that room before the end of the season and one of them goes to the district he lives in, and if he signs it his hand will be at the foot of it, and he will not be told, and there is nothing in this empire that would tell him.* Chapter 0324, still open, and the season is still on. Payoff target: Movement 5.
14. **The two people in their boxes, and the four who cannot read a paragraph, and the two of seventeen.** All unchanged by this batch and all carried at the same figures. **Nobody asked either of the two about anything, the form that put each of them there was not asked to be void by anybody, none of the four was asked one question about a number, the man with the chain said out loud that he is not one of the four, and the girl of seventeen and the reader of seventeen are unspoken to, unthanked and unsent for.** Payoff target: the fourth movement for the two of seventeen, and em dash for the rest.
15. **The bill at Lowcross, at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable.** Named once in this batch, in Chapter 0321, on the list the man of about thirty-four goes through about once in four weeks, and not again. He did not pay it and is not the reason it is unpaid. Payoff target: —.
16. **The guarantee, the bank, and about nineteen people behind it.** A guarantee still standing offered and unanswered on about four hundred and forty foot of bank with about sixty children under sixteen inside it, not in anybody's own words and with no child named. About nineteen people are still behind that bank who said no again in the same words with the same reasons. Payoff target: —.
17. **CLOSED BY THIS BATCH: the phase prompt's calendar table for this batch, and the eight-day defect it named in the outline.** Chapter 0320 is the second day of the second week of the fifth month and the ten days of this batch are seven-day steps from it, which puts Chapter 0323 on the second day of the first week of the sixth month — the date the outline's escalation item three already gives it. **The conflict the prompt named therefore does not exist in the outline and the table that creates it is the prompt's own. `outline/volume-07.md` was not amended, no closed chapter was opened, and this phase names itself as the phase that found it.** Payoff target: Chapter 0331, the seventh day after Chapter 0330, which is the second day of the first week of the eighth month.
18. **CLOSED BY THIS BATCH, and it was inherited unresolved: the day of the week on which he came the last two times.** **No chapter of this batch asserts that the third visit fell on the same day of the week as the first two, because the page gives no day of the week for either of the first two and closed Chapter 0310 gives only a lane in a month.** No chapter prints a weekday name and no chapter prints the word `schedule`. Payoff target: em dash.
19. **OPEN, and this phase found it and did not repair it: the residual in the date of the question in the second of the eleven books.** Closed Chapters 0260 and 0295 agree with each other and with the corrected outline on the fourth day of the third week of the ninth month of the year after next, and closed Chapter 0295 also says the question has been on that shelf about fifty-eight weeks, which from that date is the eleventh month of the fifth year, while Chapter 0295 is itself dated the eleventh month of the fourth year. **The bar on stating an elapsed period was lifted on the evidence that the two sources agree, and they agree about the date and not about the figure. No chapter of this batch prints a period from that question, and the reason is a residual in closed prose that this batch may not fix.** Payoff target: a repair phase on closed Chapters 0260, 0295 or 0292, which this project does not currently schedule.
20. **The two names of one building, and it is still not decided.** *Hired grain store* and *hired stone store* stand on the page for one building at the top of that lane. This batch used one name and never both, and Chapter 0327's *the store* is the same building as Chapter 0326's *hired stone store*. Nobody has been told which is right. Payoff target: —.
21. **The count of askings, and it is seven, and this batch did not move it.** **The batch prints no figure for it anywhere in its ten files.** Not one question mark in those ten files is put to a person about a person; every one of them is about a passage, a question on a shelf, a page, a number four, the fourth of four lines, a lamp, a nail, a figure or a rack. Payoff target: Chapters 0346 to 0348.
22. **The four distances a person could be findable, and none of them was walked.** The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times. The nine miles is walked once, in Chapter 0323, by the man of about sixty-one, on his own two feet, on his own time, on a day he is not paid for, and nobody sent him. **The man of about thirty-four has walked once and his distance is not yet on the page.** A stair four miles off was climbed zero times. Payoff target: —.
23. **The mechanical layer, and its two standing faults.** The fifteen-word window is still not in this project's standing check list and this is the fourth consecutive record to say so, and it is the only one of the three sweeps that found anything in this batch. **And the repair of this batch added a third fault to this row and did not repair it: the same window, run against the three hundred and twenty closed chapters, returns 141 maximal verbatim runs of fifteen words or more — one at thirty-eight words, which is the required recurrence, four at thirty-seven, the rest between fifteen and thirty-six — and every one of them is this volume's own furniture, a room described twice because it is the same room, a fact restated in a new room, a formula of the finding. The batch's governing bound is the forty-word window and it returns zero. The figure also moves with the tokenizer, 1,064 distinct tuples on the record's own method and 1,176 on a case-sensitive whole-file slide, and that instability is the same unnamed-joint defect the emphasis measure has.** The conservative superset called forty-four words names thirty-eight distinct ones and names two of them twice, and its claim of a zero is a fact about batches and not about the manuscript. Payoff target: whoever maintains that list, and it is not a writer's job.

# VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — THE THREADS AS THEY STAND AFTER CHAPTERS 0331 TO 0340

**Written by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. Nothing above this point is rewritten. A thread is closed only when the page closes it, and this batch closed none.**

## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004, AT CHAPTER 340 — THE NUMBERING RECONCILED, AND SEVEN ITEMS, SIX OF THEM OPEN

**This section is appended downward and nothing above it is edited, which is the standing rule for this file, and the two lines above it that carry a wrong identification of a man are therefore still there and are corrected here rather than overwritten. The reader who takes a fact from this file takes it from the newest section, and the fact corrected is named with the line it corrects so that a writer who lands on the old line can find this one.**

**The numbering, and why this section exists in part to fix it.** Batch 0004's writing run numbered its own additions eleven, twelve, thirteen and fourteen in `state/current.md` and wrote thirteen, fourteen, fifteen and sixteen into this ledger, and the room-merge defect was therefore called both *thirteen* in the header and *fifteen* here, and two unrelated things were both *thirteen* and both *fourteen*. **The ledger is the series and the header now prints the ledger's numbers. Items 13, 14, 15 and 16 stand as they were. Items 17 to 23 are added here, and 17 is the writing run's own fourth item, which the ledger never received, and 18 and 19 are the two lifted passages the header used to reach as thirteen and fourteen.**

17. **A card's `about nine` budget was wrong in nine of ten chapters of Batch 0004, and a plan transcribed into a figure is a figure a later writer inherits as a fact. OPEN, and the cure is standing and is not a repair: write field nine of a card from the file after the file is written, and print the figure the card promised beside the figure the file measures, and neither of the two is the governed column — the counted column is.** The proof that the cure is the right one arrived inside the same batch: when four of its chapters were repaired, every figure its own closing record had printed went stale, including the batch total, the sentence maxima, both emphasis columns, the `about nine` columns and the fifteen-word figures, and each one had to be re-measured and the old one named beside the new. **A figure a later writer inherits as a fact is a figure a later writer may have to re-measure, and that is the cost, printed.**

18. **Chapter 0333 lifted fifty-eight words out of Chapter 0327, and the shared phrasing is on the page in both. OPEN, and it is not repaired here.** It was found by the forty-word window and not by the fifteen-word one, and it was rewritten as a fresh sentence carrying the same fact and not cut, and the two chapters still stand close enough that a window finds them. **No phase may reopen Chapter 0327 to separate them, and the standing rule that the fix for a lifted passage is never deletion holds.**

19. **Chapter 0335 lifted its own opening, forty-nine words, out of Chapters 0302 and 0329, and the shared phrasing is on the page in all three. OPEN, and not repaired here, and for the same reason and with the same rule.** **These two items are the whole of what the writing run's phrase *the first two are open items thirteen and fourteen* was reaching, and they are why that phrase had to be renumbered rather than merely moved.**

20. **The outline of record gives the man of about thirty-four the third time in the rail room at the climax, and Chapter 0323 says he had not been in that room before, and the two cannot both be true. OPEN, AND THE PAGE GOVERNS, AND `outline/volume-07.md` WAS NOT AMENDED.** The page is three sentences and they agree: Chapter 0323 lists him among the people in the room as *a man of about thirty-four who had not been in the room before*; Chapter 0323 also says his two earlier rooms were the counter at the top of four flights four miles off and that *neither of those rooms is this one*; and Batch 0003's own record in `state/continuity.md` says *the man of about thirty-four was in that room, once*. **So the climax visit is the second. Chapter 0340 has been corrected from the third to the second, the phrase *the two thirds are not one third* has been struck from it because one of the two thirds was never a third, and the Batch 0005 prompt has been corrected in three places so the next writer is not handed a figure by a document that is not the page. The outline's own ninth-mile line makes the same arithmetic error and is recorded against the same item, and the outline is a file this phase has no authority to amend, which is the same standing as item 15.** The repair also had to settle a second figure for the same beat: the outline gives the reason-speech as *about nine sentences*, Chapter 0340 said *about four minutes before it*, and Chapter 0323's precedent of five sentences was a different speech, given afterwards and out of the room. **Chapter 0340 now says about nine sentences, which is the outline's figure and the prompt's figure, and the three are reconciled at the foot of `state/current.md`.**

21. **Batch 0004 gave one debt to two men inside the same ten chapters: the half of a foreman's sentence that Rennick Adley has wanted the rest of since the third month of the year after was also given, in Chapter 0331, to the man of about thirty-four with the fair hand. OPEN, AND REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS, AND RECORDED HERE BECAUSE NO TEST IN THE STANDING LIST CAN SEE THE CLASS OF IT.** The blind spot is the finding and it is written beside the result: **both men are separately and correctly on the page, both are described with a fair hand, the man of thirty-four is thirty-four or thereabouts and Adley is thirty-eight, neither is named at the point of the debt, and the distance in Chapter 0331 — *the asking is not a thing that can be done from four hundred yards* — matched neither man's position, because the man of thirty-four was standing about a hundred yards from the bay and Adley's counting room is four hundred yards further up the same lane.** Nothing in the battery looks at who holds a debt. The rule for a later writer: **in every chapter, name the holder of every debt by the thing that distinguishes him and not by his trade, and check each one against the ledger, because a debt given to the wrong man is the same kind of damage as a sentence lifted out of another chapter and it moves a fact about a person rather than a fact about a room.**

22. **A narrator's reference to the volume survives a card-language sweep keyed on the string *the volume*, because *in this volume* does not contain it. OPEN IN PART, REPAIRED IN PART.** Batch 0004 declared *the volume* at zero in all ten files and its sense sweep passed beside it, and Chapter 0336 and Chapter 0340 both said *in this volume*; the repair has reworded both and both files are clean. **A third site stands in Batch 0003's Chapter 0326 and is not this batch's to fix and is not fixed. The cure is the phrase list and not the sweep: a phrase is added to the standing list the moment it is found, whatever the sweep that found it was keyed on, and the list now carries *this volume*, *in this volume*, *this chapter*, *in this chapter*, *this batch*, *this matter*, *this page* and *the chapter*, and the number of forms is printed beside the list. Volume 07's count of these sites is three against one in the whole of Volume 03, so the practice is growing and not being caught, and that is why the list rather than the sweep is the standing answer.**

23. **Chapter 0340 catalogues the staging of the climax it hands on. OPEN, AND NAMED, AND NOT REPAIRED, AND THE PLOT IS NOT CHANGED.** The chapter sets out the rail, the four hundred yards, the nine minutes, the clerk who asks nothing and writes nothing, about four other people, the woman with the scuttle, and the refusal itself. **This is the planned shape of a movement that hands on and the volume outline puts the climax in Chapters 0346 to 0348, and the plan is not amended here; the defect is not a rule breach and it is a real constraint, and the constraint is the whole point of this item. Chapters 0346 to 0348 must execute a scene whose every element has already been named one chapter before the batch begins, and the cure is craft and not surgery: write the room, the flags, the rail and the nine minutes, and let the reader find the refusal in the mouth rather than in a list.** **The second half of the item is the finding. The room the outline specifies for the climax is one clerk and about four other people, and Chapter 0323's room held about nine, of whom three were the clerks of the building; the drift is unremarked anywhere on the page and it is named here so that Batch 0005 either holds to the outline's room or says in its closing record why it did not.**

24. **The debts, which this repair does not touch, which are the same four, and the number of them is not a fifth.** An owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003. The review gate, which opened on this batch and **fell back to the writing agent again, so that the findings this repair applied were written by a second hand and applied by a third and it is still not a review and no debt is discharged by it.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, and the items Batch 0001, 0002, 0003 and 0004 added, and the 141 runs that Batch 0003's repair added, and the seven this section adds at items 17 to 23.

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005, AT CHAPTER 350 — THE LEDGER AFTER THE FIFTH MOVEMENT, AND FIVE ITEMS THIS BATCH ADDS, ONE OF THEM A REPAIR ITEM ON CLOSED PROSE

**This section is appended downward and nothing above it is edited, which is the standing rule for this file. Where a fact above is wrong, it is corrected here and named.**

**Item 24, and it is the finding of the whole batch, and it is about measurement and not about the page.** **The closing batch of a volume is where a sweep is most likely to be skipped, and the reason is that a close reads like a summary and a summary is not a place anybody looks for a lifted sentence.** This batch's forty-word window returned eleven maximal runs on its first pass against Chapters 001 to 340, in nine of its ten chapters, and **every one of them was its own prose carried out of a closed chapter: the counting room's opening, the passage's opening, the door and the step, the clerk's room and her four thousand entries, the book-keeper's two sentences about himself, the man of thirty-four's two sentences about himself, the carrier up the passage, the second gang's count, and the closing formula's neighbours.** The governing bound is the forty-word window and it is now zero, and the sixty-word window was one and is now zero, and **not one of the eleven was found by the fifteen-word window, which returned six to eighteen per file, and not one was found by the duplicate-paragraph test at twelve words, which returned a clean zero on all ten files while eleven of them stood.** The cure is written into the card file this batch wrote before Chapter 0341 and it is the standing one: no room described twice, no person introduced twice, a figure given once and referred to afterwards by a thing, the date line once per chapter, and a canon fact written fresh. **A duplicate-paragraph test at twelve words returns zero on a batch that has lifted a paragraph and reworded its first sentence, and that is the blind spot of that test and it is now written down.**

**Item 25, OPEN, AND A REPAIR ITEM ON CLOSED PROSE, AND THIS BATCH FIXED NONE OF IT.** **Chapter 0340 says that the man of about sixty-one has already had his visit *in the second month*, and the page says the sixth month.** Chapter 0323 gives its own date as the second day of the first week of the sixth month and has the man of sixty-one say out loud that he said the whole of it once before, *in the second month, standing where you are standing*. **So there are three visits: the first is undated on the page, the second is in the second month, and the third is the sixth month, and Chapter 0340's line attaches the figure of the third to the month of the second.** The damage is small and it is real, because a chapter that is about a man who is not asked anything is damaged most by a wrong month, and a month is the only thing in this volume that a reader can check. **Chapter 0340 is a finished chapter of a completed batch and no phase may reopen it, and the chapter was not corrected here, and the ten files of this batch print no month for that man's coming at all.** `outline/volume-07.md` is not amended either, and it carries the same error in the same form, and this phase has no authority over it.

**Item 26, OPEN, AND IT IS THE SAME CLASS AS ITEM TWENTY-ONE, WHICH IS ABOUT A DEBT, AND THIS ONE IS ABOUT A FIGURE.** **The canon says a man of about sixty-one has come to that rail room three times in nineteen years, and this batch's Chapter 0346 puts him in the room a second time on the page without a fourth.** The honest construction, and the one the batch prompt required and the card file settled before the first chapter, is that the chapter does not date his coming and does not count his visits: one of the two men walked the nine miles that morning and the other walked it in a month that is gone, and the one that did it that morning is not going to say which. **So the figure of three is neither raised nor lowered on the page, and the ambiguity is in the room and not in a record, which is the only place this matter can put one.** The rule for the Volume 07 close and for Volume 08: **a canon figure that counts visits, turns, non-uses or arrivals is not a figure a chapter may confirm or deny, and the only safe sentence is one that names the figure the man himself has in his head and stops there.**

**Item 27, OPEN, AND IT IS ABOUT THE METHOD AND NOT THE PAGE, AND IT IS THE FIFTH CONSECUTIVE RECORD TO SAY IT.** **The fifteen-word window is still not in this project's standing check list and it is the only one of the three sweeps that has found anything in four consecutive batches, and this batch it returned 6 / 13 / 8 / 7 / 6 / 12 / 18 / 8 / 12 / 14 maximal runs against Chapters 001 to 340, which is 104 runs, of which ten are the required year-name date line and ninety-four are narrative furniture.** The governing bound is the forty-word window and it is zero, the sixty-word window is zero, and both are zero two words lower. **The floor of about one run per chapter is a fact about the calendar lock and not about the prose, and a check that expects a zero from this volume is asking for the calendar lock to be broken.** The unit is named beside every figure: counted as MAXIMAL runs of fifteen words or more, each counted once at its start, against the same ten files and the same three hundred and forty chapters.

**Item 28, OPEN, AND IT IS A FINDING ABOUT THE PHRASE LIST, AND IT IS THE ANSWER TO OPEN ITEM TWENTY-TWO.** **The phrase *in this matter* returns ten times across this batch's ten files, once in each, and every one of the ten is the volume's own closing formula — *the count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is the same as it was at the hour* — which stands on the page in Chapter 0323 and in every chapter of Batch 0004.** A phrase sweep keyed on the head returns ten and a reader is entitled to read that as a breach, and **the finding is that the closing formula is a recurrence of a required page line and is therefore not card language, and that the cure is a list with exemptions named and not a list of heads.** Nine other uses of *in this matter* in this batch's first draft were not the formula and were reworded, and one — a narrator's *the whole of what this matter has been about for six volumes* in Chapter 0343 — was card language by the sense and by the string and was the only true breach in the batch, and the phrase list gets one more entry: ***the whole of what this matter has been about for six volumes***, and the same for chapters, and the same for batches. **A list of heads finds a required recurrence and a list of phrases finds the real thing, and both are needed and neither is a substitute for reading.**

**Threads this batch moved.** The count of askings: seven, unmoved, and printed in none of the ten files. The man of thirty-four: the room nine miles off, walked for the second time, the asking not made, said out loud why, and not told to anybody afterwards, and the nine miles is now on the page for the first time in the volume. The man of about fifty-five with a chain: he used findability once, at a shut gate, and nobody asked him and he said nothing, and the figure of about four years is unchanged. The man of about sixty-one: present in the room, not asked, not thanked, not answering, and undated. The clerk of about fifty-five: nothing written, nothing asked, not told, and a thing she said out loud in the ninth month that she cannot check. Halla Wray: the second sentence still owed, still not asked for. Rennick Adley: the paper still at the bottom of the box, the column still without a heading, the four hundred blanks not given to him, the Lowcross bill named once and not paid. Tamsin Rook: a correct non-use entered, the box still under the counter, the fourth thing on the board still unaccounted for, the decision still unmade. Nell Kest: a wage held, a notice not sent, two women still unreachable, and a decision not to ask that is new and is hers. The woman of about twenty-six: the figure gone up, not given, no heading, and a new structural fact, that the rail room is not on the passage's work. The two of seventeen: unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, not going to start. Marn Ottery: on the page once, in her own chapter, with the box and the fourth item, and not thanked.

**Threads this batch did not touch.** The who. The second half of the question. The two documents. The sealed pages. The bundle. The substituted wording and its four ages. The quarterly return. The other return of the eleventh of the tenth. The counting-house mark. The ninety-second leaf. The Venn ruling's schedule. The hiring store's two names. The engagement's two datings. The Chapter 0145 dating phrase. The four-page item. The guarantee's own words. The forty-four-word superset and its thirty-eight distinct words. The sentence bound as a whole.

**The debts, which this batch does not touch and does not reduce, and the number of them is the same four and is not a fifth.** An owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three. The review gate, which fell back to the writing agent the last time it opened, **and which was not opened on this batch and did not return findings, and that is not an improvement and it is not a regression and it is a fact about the run rather than about the prose.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, and the items Batches 0001, 0002, 0003 and 0004 added, and the five this section adds at items twenty-four to twenty-eight.

## THE REVIEW REPAIR OF VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — ITEMS TWENTY-NINE TO THIRTY-THREE, ADDED DOWNWARDS, AND NOTHING ABOVE THIS LINE IS REWRITTEN

**This section is written by the phase that reviewed Batch 0005 and repaired it. Items 1 to 28 stand as they stood and none of their statuses is changed here. Two of the five new items are repairs, one is a repair item on prose no phase may reopen, one adds a bound to the standing list, and one is a finding about the review gate itself and it is not a repair of anything.**

**Item 29, RAISED BY THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0005, REPAIRED IN TWO FILES AND ONE HANDOFF, AND STILL OPEN IN ONE CLOSED CHAPTER.** **Chapter 0350 placed a woman of twenty in a box about nine miles off a building off the old river road, which merges her with the clerk of about fifty-five and moves her about four hundred and thirty miles out of her own counting room, and the Batch 0005 prompt forbade that in terms, and four entries in `state/character-state.md` and Chapter 0344 keep the two apart on purpose, and the origin is `outline/volume-07.md`, which places a woman of twenty about nine miles off that building and gives her a wage held.** Repaired: Chapter 0350 now places her about four hundred and thirty miles up this river in a furnace town and says in the same paragraph that the nine miles off that building is a man of about sixty-one and is neither of the two women; Chapter 0344's stacked locative was written again so that the building is named as the one off the old river road and the woman in it is at the end of the cold passage; and `workspace/volume-07/volume-close/PROMPT.md`, which the same run wrote and which had formalised the merge as settled canon for the close, has been corrected in one place and the correction is now the bullet the close must say. **NOT REPAIRED AND CARRIED FORWARD: closed Chapter 0301 prints the same merged line, and it is a completed batch's chapter of Batch 0001, and the standing rule from items 15 and 18 is that no phase reopens a closed chapter, so the collision survives in one file of the manuscript and is recorded rather than tidied. The rule for Volume 08, and it is item 15's rule with a second instance: where a plan, a prompt or an earlier chapter puts two people the state layer keeps apart in one locative, the page beats all three, the defect is a repair item naming the phase that found it, and the merge is not carried into a handoff as settled fact.** Payoff target: —.

**Item 30, RAISED BY THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0005, ADDED TO THE STANDING CHECK LIST, AND IT IS THE FIRST BOUND THAT MEASURES A BATCH AGAINST ITSELF.** **Five of Batch 0005's ten chapters closed on two byte-identical thirty-four-word clauses — Chapters 0343, 0344 and 0348 on one, Chapters 0345 and 0349 on the other — and no standing bound could see it, because every window in the list is measured against the closed page and a batch that repeats itself has lifted nothing from anywhere.** Batch 0004 used ten different closings, so this is a step up and not a constant, and the same batch's own item 24 correctly identifies this blind spot for lifted material and not for intra-batch sentence reuse. The bound added: **shared runs inside the ten files against each other, leave-one-out, at thirty-four words, with a duplicate-whole-paragraph test at twelve words beside it and neither substituting for the other.** It returned seven before the repair and returns two after, **and the two that remain are named and are not defects: they are the same man in the same room over a chandler's shop in Chapters 0345 and 0349, four weeks apart, where the recurrence is the volume's method and cutting it would cost the ordinary week the volume is built on.** The five closings were each written again as a fresh sentence carrying the same fact, the asking-count did not move, and the ten weeks did not move. **The blind spot is named beside the result: this bound is blind to a deliberate recurrence, and a bound with a standing exemption has to print the exemption, which is item 28's answer applied to a measurement and not to a word.** Payoff target: —.

**Item 31, RAISED BY THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0005, REPAIRED IN ONE FILE, AND OPEN AS A REPAIR ITEM ON CLOSED PROSE.** **The two figures a reader can check off this volume's page are an interval and a sentence count, and Batch 0005 had three values for the first and the wrong value for the second.** *The interval.* Chapter 0347 said the nine minutes were **four days** behind her; the batch's own card said **three days**; the card's own title for that chapter said *the same week*; and the calendar puts Chapter 0347 exactly one seven-day step after Chapter 0346, so the page now says **seven days** and the card says seven days and the card's title is *The Same Room, The Afternoon Of The Week After*. The 4/4 to 2/1 day-name jump at each month boundary is this volume's inherited four-week cycle, is identical across Chapters 0301 to 0350, and is not a defect and was not touched; **the nine-sevens arithmetic the writing run printed is inherited and is not what the reader can check, and the checkable figure was the one that was wrong.** *The sentence count.* Chapter 0346 said the reason-speech was **in about nine sentences** and the speech is nine quoted paragraphs carrying **thirteen** sentences, and the page now says thirteen. **The nine came from `outline/volume-07.md` and out of Batch 0004's own repair, and it is still printed in closed Chapter 0340, which says the man is going to say it *in about nine sentences*, so the two figures now stand apart on the page, Chapter 0340 is not reopened, and the divergence is carried forward here unrepaired rather than reconciled by picking one.** Payoff target: —.

**Item 32, RAISED BY THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0005, REPAIRED IN ONE FILE, AND IT IS NOT AN ITEM ABOUT FIGURES.** **Chapter 0347 said a man of about thirty-four was in the rail room *and she was in that room and she wrote none of it*, and said two lines later that she is not told that he was there, and a woman cannot be in a room and untold of it in the same page.** The second statement is the one the rest of the volume is built on — a woman who said in the ninth month that he is not coming back, and who cannot learn whether that was true, and there is no form anywhere that would let a clerk of an office be told either thing — **so the first statement was the error and the paragraph now says she was in her own room at the top end of that passage at the time of it and wrote none of it, which is the volume's own phrase for not entering a minute and is what the chapter meant.** The beat that follows is untouched and nothing about the plot moved. **The standing instruction is the one this project has had since item 22 and it is a phrase list and not a sweep: a chapter that contradicts itself about a presence cannot be caught by any bound in the list, and it was found by a person reading two adjacent paragraphs.** Payoff target: —.

**Item 33, RAISED BY THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0005, AND IT IS ABOUT THE GATE AND IT IS NOT REPAIRED BY ANYTHING A WRITER CAN DO.** **A review of Batch 0005 exists and returned nine findings, one of them serious, and it reached this phase as a run log rather than as a filed review, and `reviews/` still holds three files and every one of them carries a line saying it is not independent because the gate fell back to the writing agent.** The writing run's own closing record had said the gate *was not opened on this batch at all* and had named that as a fact about the run and not about the prose, **and that sentence is now out of date in the other direction and is superseded by this item rather than edited, because a record is not corrected by being contradicted in a later file and left standing.** What is owed and is not discharged: **a filed review for this batch in `reviews/`, in the register's own form and naming whether it was independent, which this phase cannot certify because it did not run the review and `logs/` is gitignored; and a second reading of this repair by a run that did not make it, which is the same debt as Volume 07 Batch 0001's and is now two batches of it.** The other four debts are untouched by this section: the owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, the owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, the gate, and the open items that are not plot, **and the number of items has moved from twenty-eight to thirty-three and the number of debts is still four and is still not a fifth.** Payoff target: —.

**Threads this repair moved.** The count of askings: seven, unmoved, and printed in none of the ten files and in no state file. The two women in their boxes: no longer merged in Chapter 0350 and no longer merged in the handoff, and still merged in closed Chapter 0301, which is item 29 and is not repaired. The interval between the nine minutes and the clerk's afternoon: seven days, and it was four. The reason-speech: thirteen sentences on the page and nine in a closed chapter, and the two are item 31 and are not reconciled. The five duplicate closings: five distinct wordings. Chapter 0347's own contradiction: gone. The Lowcross bill: unchanged at nineteen pounds three and fourpence, unpaid, nobody liable, named once. The clause in the second of the eleven books: still a question, still no period, its author still not asked a second time. The figure at the end of the cold passage: still in a woman's head, still not handed on, still without a heading. The guarantee: still offered, still unanswered, still unprinted in its own words, no child named. About nineteen people still behind the bank. The four who cannot read a paragraph still unasked about a number, and the man of about fifty-five with a chain and the man of about sixty-one still neither of them one of them. The two of seventeen still unspoken to, unthanked, unsent for, and not going to start.

**Threads this repair did not touch.** The who. The second half of the question. The two documents. The two sealed pages and the two refusals between them. The bundle. The substituted wording and its four ages. The quarterly return and the other return of the eleventh of the tenth. The counting-house mark. The ninety-second leaf. The Venn ruling's schedule. The hiring store's two names. The engagement's two datings. The Chapter 0145 dating phrase. The four-page item. The conservative superset of forty-four words. Items 1 to 28 in full, including item 25's month on closed prose and item 26's canon figure of visits.

---

# THE CLOSE OF VOLUME 07, AT CHAPTER 350 — THE LEDGER AT THE CLOSE, THE THIRTY-THREE ITEMS THAT WERE STANDING RESTATED, NINE ADDED, AND WHAT A CLOSE MAY NOT DO

*Appended downward by the run that took `workspace/volume-07/volume-close/PROMPT.md`, and nothing above this point is rewritten. **A close is where a debt is most likely to be quietly dropped, because a close feels like a finish, and this one dropped nothing and closed nothing.** It opened no chapter, wrote no prose, resolved nothing, and changed no status in any item above. **This close's own brief called the volume's accumulated items thirteen; the ledger carried thirty-three when this phase began and carries forty-two now, and the files are the authority, so all forty-two are restated below with their numbers and their statuses and the phase that raised each. The next section carries them forward into Volume 08 and that section is the one a Volume 08 writer reads.** The four debts are restated in full at the foot of this section, and this phase discharges none of them and the number of phases that have claimed to discharge one is zero.*

**The items 1 to 12, inherited from the closed page and earlier volumes, all OPEN, all UNCHANGED, all not this volume's to fix:** **1.** The dating phrase in Chapter 0145 is not repeated as established usage and is not a base for any date. **2.** The two measurements of the south end of a hired store and its two names: a chapter may use either, must use one and not two for the one building, must not put both in one chapter, and **nobody is to be told which one is right because it has not been decided.** **3.** The two datings of a reader's engagement, with the engagement not printed and no elapsed period computed from it. **4.** The four-page item is a question and not a repair. **5.** The sentence bound, which is held and not discharged — **and this close measured it and found it breached once, in Chapter 0319, which is item 34 below and does not change item 5's status.** **6.** The forty-word window over the closed volume is one and not zero. **7.** The closed records' sentence total reproduces under none of the five readings of that method. **8.** The emphasis measure has an unnamed and load-bearing joint — **and this close names joint two and reproduces a closed record's column to the hundredth under it, which is a finding and not a repair, and joint one still does not reproduce and is item 35's neighbour.** **9.** The conservative superset called forty-four words names thirty-eight distinct ones and names two of them twice, and its claim of a zero is a fact about batches and not about the manuscript — **and this close names the two, which is item 40.** **10.** The fifteen-word window is still not in the standing check list. **11.** The residual in closed Chapter 0295's figure of about fifty-eight weeks is irreconcilable with the two closed chapters that date the same question, on the strength of which a prohibition was lifted, which is why Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 each stated no period at all from that question in all of their files. **12.** The debt of a review nobody independent has ever given.

**The items 13 to 33, raised inside Volume 07, all OPEN, all restated with the phase that raised each:** **13** (Batch 0004) the count of a fifteen-word sweep moves by a factor of two and a half between two honest readings of one text, so a standing list that prints one without naming the unit is reporting a different question from the one it names — **and the close prints all three units for the volume: per-reference-file instances 1,525, distinct run texts 1,123, maximal runs 1,525, base Chapters 001 to 300, of which 130 runs are the required year-name date line.** **14** (Batch 0004) the required year-name date line is between fifteen and twenty-two words and stands once in every chapter, so a fifteen-word sweep has a floor of about one run per chapter — **and the volume's fifty chapters carry that floor, fifty uses in fifty files, measured, and the check is a fact about the calendar and not about the prose.** **15** (Batch 0004) the batch prompt gave Chapter 0334 a room belonging to somebody else, and **the standing instruction for Volume 08: where a plan, a prompt or an earlier chapter puts two people the state layer keeps apart in one locative, the page beats all three, the defect is recorded naming the phase that found it, and no phase reopens a closed chapter to separate them.** **16** (Batch 0004) the four debts, restated in full at the foot of this section. **17** (the repair of Batch 0004) a card's *about nine* budget was wrong in nine of ten chapters in one batch and four of ten in another, and the cure is to write field nine from the file after the file is written and to print the promise beside the measurement and neither is the governed column — **and the close states that Volume 07's ten cards differ from the counted column in all ten and from the raw column in eight of ten, that the writing run's own claim that four were wrong reproduces under neither reading, and that no sentence of the fifty files was cut or stretched to suit a number in a plan.** **18** (Batch 0004) Chapter 0333 lifted fifty-eight words out of closed Chapter 0327, rewritten as a fresh sentence carrying the same fact, not cut, unrepaired and unrepairable here. **19** (Batch 0004) Chapter 0335 lifted forty-nine words out of Chapters 0302 and 0329, same rule, same standing. **20** (the repair of Batch 0004) the outline of record gives the man of about thirty-four the third time in the rail room and Chapter 0323 says he had not been in that room before: **the page is the second, the page governs, Chapter 0340 has been corrected, `outline/volume-07.md` has not been amended and is wrong, Volume 07 wrote the second and wrote no first and no third.** **21** (Batch 0004) one debt given to two men inside the same ten chapters, which no test in the standing list can see — **and the rule for Volume 08 is that in every chapter the holder of every debt is named by the thing that distinguishes him and not by his trade and the whole debt table is run holder by holder before a chapter is opened.** **22** (Batch 0004) a narrator's reference to the volume survives a sweep keyed on the head, and the cure is a phrase list and not a sweep, grown from sixteen forms to twenty-three during this volume — **and its third site, Chapter 0326's *in this volume*, is unrepaired and is not this close's to repair.** **23** (Batch 0004) Chapter 0340 catalogues the staging of the climax it hands on, and the second half is the occupancy: the room the outline specifies holds one clerk and about four other people, Chapter 0323's room held about nine of whom three were the clerks of the building, **and Chapter 0346 writes the plan's figure three times and writes *about nine people* once and reconciles neither, which is item 39.** **24** (Batch 0005) the closing batch is where a sweep is most likely to be skipped, its forty-word window returned eleven lifted passages, and **the standing instruction is to run that sweep before the closing pass and not after it, and the blind spot is written down: a duplicate-paragraph test at twelve words returns zero on a batch that has lifted a paragraph and reworded its first sentence.** **25** (Batch 0005) Chapter 0340 places the third visit in the second month and Chapter 0323 dates that visit the sixth month, a month is the only thing in this volume a reader can check, Chapter 0340 is a finished chapter and is not reopened, and the ten files of Batch 0005 print no month for that man's coming at all. **26** (Batch 0005) a canon figure that counts visits, turns, non-uses or arrivals is not a figure a chapter may confirm or deny, and the only safe sentence names the figure the man himself has in his head and stops there — **and the close states the figure as the man knows it and does not confirm it.** **27** (Batch 0005) the fifteen-word window is still not in the standing check list, the fifth consecutive record to say so, and it is the only one of the three sweeps that has found anything in four consecutive batches. **28** (Batch 0005) a phrase list with exemptions named and not a list of heads, because a required recurrence of a page line reads as a breach and does not get cut. **29** (the review repair of Batch 0005) the merge of the two women, repaired in Chapter 0350, Chapter 0344 and the handoff, **not repaired in closed Chapter 0301, carried forward rather than tidied**, with item 15's rule as its standing instruction. **30** (the review repair of Batch 0005) five of ten chapters closed on two byte-identical thirty-four-word clauses, which no standing bound could see because every window is measured against the closed page; the bound added returns 2 after the repair and 7 before it, the two that remain are named and exempt, **and the close reports the shape of damage the bound is blind to — a batch agreeing with itself — and adds the volume-scale figure at item 37.** **31** (the review repair of Batch 0005) the two figures a reader can check, an interval and a sentence count: seven days against four, and thirteen sentences against the outline's nine, **both now correct on the page where a chapter could be corrected, the nine still printed in closed Chapter 0340, the two figures standing apart and carried unrepaired rather than reconciled by picking one.** **32** (the review repair of Batch 0005) Chapter 0347 said a woman was in the rail room and wrote none of it two lines above a paragraph saying she is not told he was there; repaired in one file, and found by a person reading two adjacent paragraphs, because no bound in the list can see a chapter that contradicts itself about a presence. **33** (the review repair of Batch 0005) the gate: a review of Batch 0005 exists, returned nine findings, and reached the repair as a run log rather than a filed review, `reviews/` still holds three files and every one says it is not independent, and what is owed is a filed review in the register's own form and a second reading of the repair by a run that did not make it.

**The nine this close adds, each naming this phase as the phase that found it, and each OPEN, and none of them repaired by this phase:**

**34. A closed chapter breaches the sentence bound, and the bound is item 5's standing and this is the first measured breach in this volume. Chapter 0319 carries one sentence of 116 words, which is a whole exchange in a mouth — a woman saying a thing out loud at about the third hour, a man coming up the flight in the middle of it with a sheet in his hand, a question, four answers and a man going down the stair — in one paragraph.** Measured per paragraph with no sentence carried across a join, the volume's per-file maxima run from 63 to 116, seventeen files are at or above eighty, one is over ninety and this one is of a hundred words or more. **Chapter 0319 is a completed batch's chapter, no phase may reopen it, the breach is recorded and not fixed, and the blind spot is named: a bound that counts words cannot see a sentence that is short in clauses, and one exchange is the shape that beats it.** Payoff target: a repair phase, which this project does not currently schedule.

**35. The fifteen-word window's recorded figures do not reproduce, and the window is not at fault.** Batch 0005's record printed 113 at fifteen and 138 at thirteen for its own ten files against Chapters 001 to 340. **This close's reading of that same pair of files returns 761 and 1,027, and three stated readings of the same method return 160, 181 and 154 at fifteen, so the recorded figures are reproducible under none of them; while the governing windows reproduce exactly, at sixty, forty and thirty-eight returning 0, 0 and 1, the 1 being the required recurrence in Chapter 0341.** So the defect is in the fifteen-word reading and not in the window, and the unit is named and the method is not, which is item 13 at volume scale. **The blind spot of the whole family is beside the result: a sweep is only as reproducible as the sentence it is measured in, and a record that prints a figure without printing the reading has printed something else.** Payoff target: whoever maintains the check list, and it is not a writer's job.

**36. A premise this close was handed does not hold the page, and the page's own lock held anyway.** The brief stated that no chapter of the fifty printed the count of askings except the one the outline of record prints. **It is named in seventeen of them — Chapters 0321, 0323, 0330, 0331, 0332, 0339, 0340 and 0341 to 0350 — the numeral is printed in three, 0321, 0323 and 0330, the other fourteen say it did not move or is the same as it was, the phrase stands inside bold in ten of the seventeen, 0330 and 0341 to 0349, and the word boundary sits in three files: Chapters 0324 and 0347 hold *seven days* between them three times and Chapter 0331 holds *about seven minutes* once.** The count is **seven** and it did not move, which is the lock that matters, and a chapter that prints the figure without moving it is the page working and not the page failing. **What this item is really about is a checkable premise: a close that inherits a premise about the prose instead of measuring it will state a fact about fifty files that no reader can confirm, and the correction is that the figure is named in seventeen chapters and the close prints it once and computes nothing from it.** Payoff target: the next volume's outline, if one is written. **CORRECTED BY THE REVIEW REPAIR, and the correction is the item's own point: as this item was first written it named six chapters, listed 0301, 0311 and 0312, and none of those three files contains the count of askings at all; it said four in bold where ten are; and it said nine files at a word boundary where there are four. The item was arguing that a close must not carry a premise about the prose it has not measured, and it was carrying one, and the number a reader checks first was the number that was wrong. The lock, which is that the count is seven and does not become eight, was right from the first and is untouched by the correction.**

**37. The standing intra-batch bound is blind to a volume agreeing with itself, and at the scale of the volume it is not small.** Item 30's bound — shared runs inside a batch's ten files against each other, leave-one-out, at thirty-four words — returns 2 after the repair, both named and exempt. **Run as this close ran it, each movement's ten files against the other forty, it returns 221 / 228 / 32 / 9 / 8, and the duplicate-whole-paragraph test at twelve words returns four identical paragraphs of twelve words or more inside the fifty files: a 41-word paragraph in Chapters 0303 and 0311, 27 and 20 words in Chapters 0304 and 0313, and 15 words in Chapters 0310 and 0314.** Every one of them is this volume's method, which is a room described twice because it is the same room, **and the standing instruction for Volume 08 is to run the intra-volume variant at the end of every batch and not only inside the batch, and to print the exemption with the figure, because a bound with an unstated exemption gets reported as a breach and then quietly not run.** Payoff target: the check list.

**38. A sentence was lifted whole out of a closed chapter and the governing bound cannot see it.** Chapter 0308 carries, byte for byte, a nineteen-word sentence from closed Chapter 0295: *the carrier went down the passage and the cold came up after her and the rail stood in it.* **The forty-word window is blind to it, the sixty-word window is blind to it, the fifteen-word window sees it as one of its 1,525, and the duplicate-paragraph test at twelve words is the only bound in the standing list that catches it.** This is the same class as item 24's eleven and it survived that repair because the repair ran the sweep against the closed page at forty words and nineteen is under forty. **The standing instruction is the one item 24 already carries — print the same sweep two words lower beside every result — and this item is the proof that two words was not low enough, because the damage here is nineteen words and the whole ladder has to be run, not the bottom rung.** Payoff target: a repair phase, and the check list.

**39. One room has two occupancy figures on the page in one chapter and nothing reconciles them.** Chapter 0346 writes the outline's figure — about four other people, and a clerk, and a rail — three times, and writes **about nine people** once, in the paragraph that says what was said in that room went into about nine people and not into the table and not onto the shelf. **Chapter 0323's room held about nine of whom three were the clerks of the building. The two figures stand apart in the same chapter, the close does not pick one, and this close's brief stated that the chapter printed no occupancy total at all, which is not what the file says.** Payoff target: a reader, and no phase.

**40. The conservative superset's arithmetic is settled, and it is one line long.** The list as written at item 34 of `outline/batches/volume-06-batch-0004.md` is **forty listed entries naming thirty-eight distinct words, with *docket* and *tribunal* named twice.** So the distinct count of thirty-eight reproduces, **the state rows' *thirty-nine listed entries* is one out, and the two words the record did not say can now be named.** The sweep returns zero across the fifty files of this volume for every word in it, including *register*, which is why the five-column book with a sixth ruled space at the bottom of every page of it is described as a book and kept at a clerk's window. **This closes the naming half of item 9 and repairs nothing: the closed card file is not reopened.** Payoff target: whoever maintains the list.

**41. A defect in the outline of record, found by this close, and not fixed in the file that has it.** `outline/volume-07.md`'s third movement says of the coming in Chapter 0323 that it is **the same day of the week he came the last two times.** **The calendar lock in that same file forbids any chapter to assert which day of the week anything fell on, and no chapter of the fifty does — the weekday sweep returns zero in both forms and the assertion returns zero — and an outline of record is a document a writer reads, and this one asserts in a document what a chapter is forbidden to say.** The outline is not this close's to amend, and a close does not amend the file that has the defect. **The standing instruction for Volume 08 is the lock's own: no chapter, no card, no prompt and no state file asserts which day of the week anything fell on, and the one false positive a verifier gets is the modal *may*, which is itself zero across this volume's fifty files.** Payoff target: whoever writes Volume 08's outline of record.

**42. The fifth visit figure moves on the page between two chapters a week apart and nothing dates the visit that moved it.** Chapter 0323, at the second day of the first week of the sixth month, says he has been in that room **twice** in nineteen years. Chapter 0324, at the second day of the second week of the same month, says he has stood on the wrong side of that rail **three times** in nineteen years, and Chapters 0325, 0330, 0336, 0343, 0346 and 0347 carry the three. **Nothing on the page dates the third coming and nothing reconciles the two figures except his own sentence in 0324 and 0336 — he has said the whole of it out loud twice in nineteen years, in this room on two of his three visits — which is item 26's construction working exactly as it is meant to.** The finding is the blind spot: **a figure of visits is uncheckable by a reader, because the volume's own rule forbids dating it, so the only honest sentence is the one naming what the man knows and stopping there, and a close states it and does not confirm it.** Payoff target: —.
